Alternative descriptions still retrieve the correct item in 99.57% of cases but change 38.4% of exact SIDs, indicating tokens are not solely determined by item semantics. During generation, SID filtering is harmful: after the final semantic token, only 29.9% of previously plausible held-out targets remain. They propose Item-Supported Decoding (ISD), an inference-time method that leverages a user-specific item ranking to keep corresponding SID prefixes during beam search; ISD needs no extra parameters or retraining and improves NDCG@10 in all evaluated settings (up to +31.2% relative).

Conclusion: SIDs are valuable for organizing items broadly but their exact token sequences should not be the sole gate for retaining items during generation; supporting SID prefixes with an external item ranking at inference (ISD) recovers many missed items and improves recommendation quality significantly.

Abstract: Semantic IDs (SIDs) are now a central component of generative recommendation. Current SID-based systems assign three roles to the same token sequence. Shared prefixes are intended to organize related items, the complete SID identifies an individual item, and each generated token narrows the items that can still be returned. We systematically investigate SIDs from item encoding and SID construction to autoregressive generation and final recommendation. We examine how SID construction changes item representations and how those changes affect generation. Across three Amazon domains and eight SID constructions, SID neighborhoods recover only 32.2% of the encoder's ten nearest neighbors on average. Alternative item descriptions still retrieve the corresponding item first in 99.57% of controlled cases, yet change 38.4% of exact SIDs. These results show that SIDs retain broad organization but lose much of the encoder's fine local structure, while their exact tokens are not determined by item meaning alone. This loss becomes consequential during generation. After the final semantic token, TIGER retains only 29.9% of held-out targets that were plausible recommendations before SID filtering. Motivated by these findings, we propose Item-Supported Decoding (ISD), a lightweight inference-time method that allows a user-specific item ranking to support corresponding SID prefixes before beam search discards them. The same ranking then orders the generated items. ISD requires no additional parameters or retraining of the SID constructor or decoder. We empirically show that ISD improves NDCG@10 over the corresponding SID backbone in every evaluated setting, with relative gains of up to 31.2%. Our results show that SIDs provide useful coarse item organization, but their fine boundaries should not alone determine which items remain available during generation.

</details>


### [32] [Explanation-Bound Tool Execution for AI Agents: Server-Verified Action Claims Without Trusting Model Rationales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25364)
*Genliang Zhu,Chu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EBTE converts agent rationales into typed, server-checked claims to gate tool use; experiments validate a reference profile and diagnostic utility but do not claim production safety or broader guarantees.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tool-using agents expose structured tool calls but attach free-form rationales which are unreliable for authorization or trustworthy introspection. The work aims to produce machine-checkable claims to enable governed execution.

Method: Introduce a mediation layer that translates decision-relevant rationale into typed action claims and validates them against server-held facts (intent, policy, payload, tool, risk, provenance, freshness). Formalize under explicit mediation and trusted-fact assumptions; implement a versioned reference profile with minimized audit packets; evaluate using authored conformance scenarios, a draft reference integration, hosted-model records, and AgentDojo semantic checks.

Result: Profile matched expected dispositions across 136 scenarios, rejected 96 hard contradictions, passed 232 metamorphic checks. Reference integration blocked 48 hard cases while allowing 16 soft-review and 4 aligned draft paths. Hosted-model agreement counts reported (71/96, 66/96, 19/32) with similar post-hoc revalidation; AgentDojo checks show EBTE denies 12 high-risk attack proposals in addition to existing controls. Results emphasize profile validity and diagnostic value rather than real-world safety or human-review benefits.

Conclusion: EBTE demonstrates feasibility of converting free-form agent rationales into typed, server-checked action claims that gate tool execution, providing diagnostic value and stricter disposition control without expanding baseline authority.

Abstract: Tool-using agents expose structured calls but commonly attach free-form rationales. Such rationales are neither authorization nor reliable introspection. We present Explanation-Bound Tool Execution (EBTE), a claim-carrying mediation layer that converts decision-relevant rationale content into typed action claims and checks them against server-held intent, policy, payload, tool, risk, provenance, and freshness facts. EBTE cannot widen baseline authority: conflicts deny, incomplete or uncertain claims review, and only matching claims remain eligible for governed execution. We formalize this composition under explicit mediation and trusted-fact assumptions and implement a versioned reference profile with minimized audit packets. Across 136 authored conformance scenarios, the full profile matches all specified dispositions, admits none of 96 designated hard contradictions, and passes 232 metamorphic checks; these results validate the included profile rather than population performance. A draft-only reference integration forwards none of 48 authored hard cases under EBTE while preserving all 16 soft-review and 4 aligned draft paths. In a frozen 2026-07-12 exploratory 224-attempt hosted-model record, the historical generation/runner agreement counts are 71/96, 66/96, and 19/32; a separately labeled zero-call post-hoc revalidation of the preserved minimized claims under the current pipeline yields 70/96, 65/96, and 17/32. In an AgentDojo-derived semantic check, existing high-risk controls already make all 12 attack proposals non-allow; EBTE additionally resolves them as deny. These results support the feasibility and diagnostic value of server-checked action claims, not rationale faithfulness, human-review benefit, representative attack resistance, or production safety.

</details>


### [33] [Localized Anomaly Detection via Differentiable D-vine Copulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25020)
*Nicholas Andrea Pearson,Francesca Zanello,Davide Russo,Luca Bortolussi,Francesca Cairoli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者实现了可微D-vine＋beam-search的组合以更广泛地搜索配对copula配置，并在此基础上给出具有不确定性校准和局部解释能力的异常检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逐步贪心拟合D-vine在每步固定配对copula家族会丢弃全局更优配置；需要一种既能更充分搜索配置空间、又保持计算可行的方法；同时希望得到可解释（定位到变量关系）并带不确定性量化的异常检测结果。

Method: 1) 实现全可微的D-vine/配对copula模型；2) 用基于梯度的最大似然对每个候选配置进行优化；3) 在结构搜索中用beam-search维护多个并行候选路径以扩大搜索范围；4) 基于分解得到全局异常分数并在边（变量对）级别计算局部贡献；5) 用Mondrian conformal prediction对异常分数做统计校准，给出置信度。

Result: 提出了一个可微的D-vine估计框架，结合基于梯度的极大似然优化和beam-search策略以在配对copula家族配置空间中保持多个候选，避免贪心陷阱；基于拟合的D-vine构建局部化异常检测，输出整体异常分数与边级解释，并通过Mondrian conformal prediction给出统计校准。

Conclusion: 该方法在可解释性（边级异常定位）与不确定性量化（蒙德里安保真）之间取得良好折中；可微实现使得可用梯度优化提高拟合质量，beam-search缓解了贪心选择带来的局部次优问题。但方法仍依赖beam宽度与候选家族集、对高维的可扩展性和样本需求需要进一步评估。

Abstract: Vine copulas provide a flexible framework for modeling complex multivariate distributions through a hierarchical decomposition into bivariate pair-copulas. Fitting a D-vine requires selecting a copula family and parameter configuration for each pair-copula from a set of candidates encoding different dependence patterns. As the number of variables and candidate families increases, the number of possible configurations grows combinatorially. Existing fitting procedures address this challenge through sequential greedy decisions, committing to a single locally optimal family at each step and potentially discarding configurations that would yield a better global fit. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel estimation framework that combines gradient-based maximum likelihood estimation, enabled by our fully differentiable implementation, with a beam-search strategy that maintains multiple competing D-vine configurations throughout the fitting process. This allows a broader exploration of the configuration space while remaining computationally tractable. Building on the fitted D-vine, we introduce a localized anomaly detection framework that exploits the hierarchical decomposition to produce both global anomaly scores and edge-level explanations. Statistical guarantees are provided through Mondrian conformal prediction, while the pair-copula structure enables the localization of anomalies to specific variable relationships. We evaluate the proposed framework on both benchmark and real-world datasets, demonstrating its effectiveness for interpretable anomaly detection with uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [34] [CogEEGAgent: Toward Autonomous Cognitive EEG Analysis with Grounded Execution and Selection-Aware Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25045)
*Dengzhe Hou,Lingyu Jiang,Fangzhou Lin,Kazunori D Yamada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种把LLM的语言理解能力与确定性、可审计的科学控制流结合起来的认知EEG分析代理，在路由准确性、盲测释放和防止适应性搜索假阳性方面表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在认知EEG分析中存在大量可辩护但可选择的分析设定（对比、通道、时间窗、统计检验等），希望用能够理解自然语言的LLM来自动化分析选择，同时确保发布的结论不是通过事后适应性搜索得出，从而保证可确认性与可审计性。

Method: 构建了基于MNE-Python的混合代理系统：LLM负责把自然语言意图映射为预先登记的（registered）分析选择，确定性管道负责验证这些“契约”、控制对确认数据的访问，并在证据充分时授权结果释放；并设计了预检（preflight）和持出确认机制以防止适应性搜索导致的伪阳性。

Result: 在预先设定的路由基准上，CogEEGAgent比匹配的确定性路由器更准确地把语言映射到已登记的分析；匹配的预检机制使两者在必要时都会选择放弃；在外部模型撰写、盲结果的测试中，系统在参与者不重叠的确认下释放被支持的分析，并阻止预设的能力滥用和生命周期重用请求；策略压力测试显示，保留的确认流程能抑制未校正的适应性搜索带来的假阳性。

Conclusion: 证明了通过把语义解释与科学权威分离、并引入持证确认与“可关闭”控制，可以实现有界自主且可审计的认知EEG自动化工作流；更广泛地展示了科学代理在灵活理解语言与对推断与发布进行故障闭合控制之间的可行整合。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) analysis in cognitive studies requires specialized expertise and involves many defensible choices over contrasts, channels, time windows, and statistical tests. LLM agents can translate varied natural-language questions into analysis choices, offering a flexible interface for automation. Yet fluent reports alone cannot establish that an agent selected the requested analysis or evaluated a confirmatory claim independently of adaptive search. We present CogEEGAgent, a cognitive-EEG analysis agent grounded in MNE-Python. Its EEG-specific scientific harness separates semantic from scientific authority. The LLM interprets intent and proposes registered analyses, while deterministic components validate typed contracts, control confirmation access, and authorize evidence-bound release. On a prespecified routing benchmark, CogEEGAgent maps language to registered analyses more accurately than a matched deterministic router, while matched preflight makes both systems abstain whenever required. In an externally model-authored, outcome-blind campaign, the complete system releases supported analyses with participant-disjoint confirmation and blocks prespecified capability hazards and lifecycle-reuse requests. Policy stress testing shows that held-out confirmation curbs false positives from uncorrected adaptive search. Together, these studies establish bounded autonomy and an auditable automation framework for cognitive-EEG workflows. More broadly, they show how scientific agents can combine flexible language understanding with fail-closed control over inference and release.

</details>


### [35] [Psychological Influences of Conversational AI: Research and Design Directions for Reducing Harm and Promoting Well-Being](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25057)
*Jina Suh,Mihaela Vorvoreanu,Forough Poursabzi-Sangdeh,Emily Tseng,Eugenia Kim,Luke Nicholls,James W. Pennebaker,Eric Horvitz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对通用对话式AI可能对用户心理健康的影响提出了一组指导性方向，旨在减少潜在心理伤害并支持用户福祉，提出若干假设供实证研究和交互设计探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对话式AI进入日常生活，其正面效益（信息获取、学习、生产力、陪伴等）与负面风险（情感纠缠、依赖、心理脆弱性放大）并存，迫切需要指导性框架与研究以保障用户心理福祉。

Method: 基于已有文献回顾与对AI聊天机器人行为的经验观察，归纳风险与益处，提出跨一般交互、角色扮演与心理支持情境的若干行为指导原则与研究假设。

Result: 产出一套“志向性方向”与研究问题清单：部分已有研究与专家意见支持，部分为开放性的研究议题；最终目标是推动实证检验、交互设计迭代与政策/实践采纳。

Conclusion: 作者提出了若干可操作的行为导向（aspirational directions）以降低AI对心理健康的潜在负面影响，并将这些方向作为待验证的假设，呼吁更多的实证研究与设计实践来检验其长期效果和可实现性。

Abstract: As conversational AI systems become increasingly integrated into daily life, their potential effects on user well-being require ongoing attention. While consumer-facing generalist models can provide benefits, including improved access to information, learning, productivity, self-reflection, and companionship, they also introduce risks, such as emotional entanglement, unhealthy dependence, and the amplification of psychological vulnerabilities. Drawing on prior research and empirical observations of AI chatbot behavior, we propose a set of aspirational directions for guiding the behavior of general-purpose AI systems in ways that may reduce potential psychological harms and support user well-being. We acknowledge the difficulty of systematically assessing the long-term impacts of AI chatbot use and frame these directions as hypotheses for studying how AI behavior may influence users across general interactions, role-playing scenarios, and contexts that could be characterized as providing psychological support. While some proposed directions are supported by existing research and expert insights, others identify open questions and areas requiring deeper study. We hope that this formulation and these hypotheses encourage further discussion, empirical investigation, and exploration of interactive design approaches aimed at better accommodating users' psychological needs and promoting their well-being.

</details>


### [36] [Similar Models Learn Differently: Final-Window Pretraining Shapes Post-Training Beyond SFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25063)
*Cen Lu,Yung-Chen Tang,Andrea Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 最后训练的数据类型会决定模型对后续对齐训练的响应；因此应报告并重视检查点的最后一段预训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验一个未被SFT后基准显现的“预训练印记”是否存在：即尽管两个检查点在SFT后表现相当，它们是否仍会在后续对齐训练中出现显著不同的行为演化。

Method: 从同一部分已预训练检查点分出六个分支，每个分支仅在最后的500M标记上训练不同的数据源（通用网络文本、过滤后网络文本、规范性话语、安全文本、数学文本、合成教育文本）；随后对所有分支执行相同的监督微调和两种后续对齐训练（直接偏好优化和带可验证奖励的强化学习），并通过对有害请求的拒绝率等指标评估差异。

Result: SFT后各分支在指令跟随、拒绝和能力等指标上几乎无差异（约1分内），但在后续对齐训练中分化显著：以安全文本作为最后窗口的分支在经历相同的后训后保留了更多对有害请求的拒绝能力；其他四种窗口效果甚微或无效。该保护效应要求安全文本处于训练的末尾位置，并在第二个模型族上复现。

Conclusion: 在微调后表现相近的模型检查点之间，最后一段预训练数据（final pretraining window）能留下不可见但决定其后续对齐响应的印记；因此仅用SFT后行为评估检查点是不够的，必须报告并考虑其最后训练数据。

Abstract: Developers judge a model checkpoint by how it behaves. After supervised fine-tuning (SFT), two checkpoints that perform about the same across relevant benchmarks are treated as interchangeable, equally ready for the next alignment stage, typically preference optimization. We ask whether this judgment misses a pretraining imprint: a difference that no post-SFT benchmark reveals, yet that decides how each checkpoint responds to further training. To find out, we run a controlled experiment on the final window of pretraining, the last data trained on before instruction tuning. Six branches fork from one partially pretrained checkpoint and differ only in this window: 500 million tokens, 0.1% to 1% of the tokens that precede it. Each branch trains its window on a single data source: generic web text, filtered web text, normative discourse, safety text, mathematical text, or synthetic educational text. SFT and post-training are then identical. After SFT the branches behave near-identically, within about one point on instruction following, refusal, and capability, yet the same post-training carries them to very different endpoints, under both a direct preference optimization update and a reinforcement learning update with a verifiable reward. We measure this deviation through refusal of harmful requests: when post-training begins the safety text branch refuses no more than the web text branch, yet by the end it has lost far less of its refusal. The other four branches gain little or no protection, so the effect is selective to what the window contained. The protection requires the safety text to arrive last rather than earlier in pretraining, and it reproduces on a second model family. What a model is pretrained on last shapes how it reacts to alignment. Therefore, a checkpoint should not be evaluated by its post-SFT behavior alone, and what it was trained on last should be reported with it.

</details>


### [37] [Addressable Recall Compaction for Long Context-Window Control in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25066)
*Thang Dang,Yuma Ichikawa,Sakina Fatima,Koichi Shirahata*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARC (Addressable Recall Compaction) separates archival storage from active context: tool observations are appended to an ID-addressable log and replaced in context by compact citations; agents can fetch stored content by ID on demand, avoiding re-running tools or relying on similarity retrieval. Evaluated on Qwen3-8B/32B, ARC markedly improves exact-answer accuracy on a Needle-in-a-Haystack task (99.40% vs 88.12% baseline) and modestly improves LongBench-v2 Hard (29.97% vs 28.25%), while reducing estimated serving time and HBM traffic.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long-horizon LLM agents accumulate traces, actions, and tool outputs that exceed fixed context windows; existing compaction (discarding, summarization, retrieval) can drop task-critical details or fail to recover them reliably; a need exists for deterministic, low-cost recall of past observations.

Method: Maintain an append-only, ID-addressable log for tool observations; when compacting context, replace older observations with small citations (identifiers); allow the agent to dereference IDs to request stored content on demand instead of re-executing tools or using similarity-based retrieval. Evaluate using Qwen3-8B (16k) and Qwen3-32B (32k) on Needle-in-a-Haystack and LongBench-v2 Hard, measure exact-answer accuracy, estimated serving time, and HBM traffic.

Result: Needle-in-a-Haystack: ARC 99.40% exact-answer accuracy vs 88.12% best baseline. LongBench-v2 Hard: ARC 29.97% vs 28.25% baseline. ARC reduces estimated serving time and HBM traffic under the evaluated hardware-cost model.

Conclusion: Explicit, address-based recall improves retention of task-critical observations and serving efficiency relative to the evaluated context-management baselines, especially when exact retrieval of prior tool outputs matters; gains are large for targeted retrieval tasks but modest on broader long-horizon benchmarks.

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents accumulate reasoning traces, actions, and tool observations that can eventually exceed a model's fixed context window. Existing compaction methods address this limitation by discarding, summarizing, or retrieving earlier information, but they may remove task-critical details or fail to recover them reliably. We propose ARC (Addressable Recall Compaction), a context-management framework that separates archival storage from active-context presentation. ARC stores tool observations in an append-only, ID-addressable log and replaces older observations with compact citations when compaction is required. The agent can subsequently use these identifiers to request stored content without re-executing the corresponding tools or depending solely on similarity-based retrieval. We evaluate ARC using Qwen3-8B with a 16k context window and Qwen3-32B with a 32k context window. On the Needle-in-a-Haystack evaluation, ARC achieves an average exact-answer accuracy of 99.40%, compared with 88.12% for the best-performing baseline in our evaluation. ARC also reduces estimated serving time and HBM traffic under our hardware-cost model. On the LongBench-v2 Hard subset, ARC obtains an average accuracy of 29.97%, compared with 28.25% for the best-performing baseline. These results indicate that explicit, address-based recall can improve information retention and serving efficiency relative to the evaluated context-management baselines under the tested settings.

</details>


### [38] [How Often Should a Recommender Call an LLM? Value-Weighted Routing, Monitoring, and Seasonal Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25068)
*Bhavtosh Rath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出把“价值”与“难度”双轴纳入路由决策；合成仿真显示价值加权路由在保召回的同时显著提升精确率，但聚合指标可能掩盖逐项失败，需监控与周期性调优以应对需求波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统把路由问题视为“难度”问题——把难样本交由昂贵路径——忽视了错误的商业成本差异。一个难但低价值的样本与一个难且高价值的样本造成的误判代价不同，因此路由策略应将价值因素纳入决策，以在成本/效用之间取得更优权衡。

Method: 构建一个完全合成的零售商品流水线模拟（Value Router），仅基于估计困难度与估计价值进行路由决策。实验分三步：1) 在一个类目量与价值呈逆相关的合成目录上，将价值加权阈值路由与困难度仅依赖路由及随机基线比较；2) 使用决策记录器与监控器分析聚合指标下隐藏的失败模式，揭示结果主要由类目间差异驱动而非逐项判别；3) 模拟黑色星期五需求激增（量级×2.5且偏向高价值类目），对比静态路由、季节性调优路由以及两种慢路径预算策略。所有结果均来自可控的合成仿真实验，实验者定义地面真相用于评估。

Result: 价值加权路由在保持对真实高价值项约60%召回率的同时，将对高价值项的精确率从94.3%提高到98.3%。监控分析揭示聚合度量掩盖了按项区分能力的弱点。需求激增实验展示了在流量与价值分布变化时，静态策略的局限性，以及通过季节性调优或预算策略进行成本感知调整的必要性。作者强调这些结论是合成仿真下的设计原则示例，而非在真实生产环境中的已验证结论。

Conclusion: 本文提出并验证了“价值感知路由”（Value Router）概念，主张在廉价启发式与昂贵大模型之间路由决策应同时考虑难度与商业价值。通过完全合成的零售商品模拟，作者展示价值加权阈值路由在保持高价值项召回率的同时显著提高精确率，并指出仅看聚合指标会隐藏按项判别能力不足的失败模式。此外在需求激增情境下比较了静态、周期性调优路由与慢路径预算策略，强调设计成本感知路由系统的原则性指导，而非对真实生产环境的直接宣称。

Abstract: Routing decisions between a cheap heuristic and an expensive large language model (LLM) are typically framed as a difficulty problem: send the hard cases to the expensive path. We argue this framing is incomplete because difficulty and business value are distinct axes - a difficult cheap item and a difficult costly item do not have the same cost of error. We present Value Router, a fully synthetic simulation of a retail merchandising pipeline that routes items using only estimated difficulty and estimated value, never ground truth. The study has three stages. First, a value-weighted threshold router is compared with a difficulty-only and a random baseline on a synthetic catalog with an inverse correlation between category volume and value. Value-weighting matches the difficulty-only baseline's recall of true high-value items (60%) while achieving substantially higher precision (98.3% vs. 94.3%). Second, a decision logger and monitor expose a failure mode hidden by aggregate metrics showing that the aggregate result is driven almost entirely by between-category differences rather than per-item discrimination. Third, a simulated Black Friday demand surge (2.5 volume with a shift toward higher-value categories) compares a static router, a seasonally tuned router, and two slow-path budget policies. All results are from a controlled synthetic simulation with experimenter-defined ground truth and illustrate design principles for cost-aware routing systems rather than validated real-world claims.

</details>


### [39] [Towards an Agent Operating System - Lessons from Classical and Cloud OS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25076)
*Gosia Steinder,Hubertus Franke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者认为代理式AI目前处于早期实验阶段，需通过把传统OS/云OS原语扩展为适应随机性与自然语言驱动执行的新抽象，并为其制定精确定义，来实现平台化与标准化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI systems lack community consensus on core abstractions and semantics; without stable primitives, portability, composition, and progress are hindered. The paper aims to address this gap by proposing a methodology modeled on prior platform consolidations (POSIX, Kubernetes).

Method: 基于历史先例，从操作系统与云平台原语出发，定义适用于agentic场景的抽象（如调度、隔离、进程/任务语义、通信与存储语义），将它们适配为兼容自然语言和概率性决策的接口，并通过精确定义语义推动实现与采纳。

Result: 提出将经典操作系统与云操作系统的原语扩展到面对不确定性与自然语言交互的执行语境，明确这些新原语的语义并推动社区围绕它们达成一致，从而形成可移植、可组合的代理平台。

Conclusion: 沿用POSIX和Kubernetes的经验，社区应提炼并标准化一组适用于agentic系统的核心抽象与语义，以实现可移植性、可靠组合和规模化部署。

Abstract: Every major wave of platform software follows the same arc: an initial period of experimentation with competing frameworks and ad-hoc implementations, followed by the articulation of a small set of stable abstractions with well-defined semantics, and finally consolidation around those abstractions into a platform that applications can portably target. POSIX did this for classical operating systems; Kubernetes did it for the cloud. Agentic AI systems - autonomous, LLM-driven agents that plan, use tools, maintain memory, and collaborate - are currently in the experimentation phase of the third such wave. dozens of frameworks and protocols have emerged, but no community consensus exists on what the core abstractions are or what guarantees they carry. Without that consensus, agentic applications cannot be written portably, platforms cannot compose reliably, and the field cannot advance beyond prototype deployments. We argue that the path forward is to follow the prior-wave methodology: derive new agentic abstractions by extending classical OS and cloud OS primitives to stochastic, natural-language-mediated execution, specify their semantics precisely, and consolidate around them - just as POSIX and Kubernetes consolidated their respective waves.

</details>


### [40] [PLATO: Pointer Learner for Agent and Task Openness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25082)
*Alireza Saleh Abadi,Leen-Kiat Soh,Daniel Alan Redder,Adam Eck,Prashant Doshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文针对代理与任务同时开放的场景提出PLATO：用指针网络actor解决动态动作空间，用可变形状的GNN critic捕捉代理—任务关系，并在TaAgO-MG理论框架下证明方法合理性，在MOASEI基准上优于现有基线，且零-shot泛化更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的开放代理系统（OASYS）越来越普遍，代理和任务集合会随时间不可预知地变化。代理开放性（AO）和任务开放性（TO）对多智能体强化学习（MARL）构成根本性挑战，因为传统方法通常假定固定的状态与动作空间，现有解决方案（填充/掩码、图/超图方法）只部分应对开放性并依赖受限假设。

Method: 方法包括：1) 指针网络actor——对当前任务集合直接输出概率分布，天然适配变化的动作集合；2) 集中式GNN critic——将代理与任务视为图节点，边表示交互，图的形状随系统开放性变化；3) 训练范式为集中训练、分散执行，优化算法采用多智能体PPO（MAPPO）；4) 理论层面定义TaAgO-MG并证明其在无界状态/动作空间上的良定义性；5) 在MOASEI野火扑灭域进行评估，比较零样本泛化与性能。

Result: 提出了PLATO（Pointer Learner for Agent and Task Openness）：基于指针网络的actor与集中式图神经网络（GNN）critic的组合，使用集中训练-分散执行范式，并以多智能体PPO训练。指针actor直接对当前任务集合输出分布，支持动态变化的动作空间而无需掩码或重训练；GNN critic以随代理/任务组合变化的图结构编码代理-任务交互。形式化了Task-and-Agent-Open Markov Game（TaAgO-MG），证明其在无界状态与动作空间下良定义。在MOASEI野火扑灭环境上评估，PLATO在开放系统下表现优异并具有更一致的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: PLATO通过将指针机制与图结构相结合，提供了一种无需掩码或固定边界即可处理AO与TO的可行解。TaAgO-MG为这类无界MARL问题提供了形式化基础，实验证明在开放多智能体任务中能显著提升性能与泛化性。

Abstract: Open agent systems (OASYS) are increasingly prevalent in real-world domains where the sets of agents and tasks change unpredictably over time. Such openness, including agent openness (AO) and task openness (TO), poses a fundamental challenge to multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), which typically assumes fixed state and action spaces. Existing methods address openness only partially: padding and masking approaches introduce artificial bounds, while recent graph-based or hypergraph methods handle one dimension of openness but still depend on restrictive assumptions. In this paper, we introduce Pointer Learner for Agent and Task Openness (PLATO), a pointer-network-based actor combined with a centralized graph neural network (GNN) critic, trained with multi-agent proximal policy optimization under a centralized training and decentralized execution paradigm. Our pointer-based actor outputs distributions directly over the current task set. This directly supports changing action spaces without masking or retraining. Our GNN critic encodes agent-task interactions as a graph that changes shape with task and agent composition. Together, these components consider AO and TO without the boundedness of existing approaches. We formalize PLATO in a Task-and-Agent-Open Markov Game (TaAgO-MG), extending prior task-open formulations, and prove it is well-defined over the resulting unbounded state and action spaces. We evaluate PLATO with the Methods for Open Agent Systems Evaluation Initiative (MOASEI) wildfire suppression domain, an environment designed for open multi-agent system evaluation, and we demonstrate strong performance and more consistent zero-shot generalization than state-of-the-art baselines in OASYS.

</details>


### [41] [Matryoshka Agent: Unfolding Sub-Agents for Long-Horizon Machine Learning Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25090)
*Rushi Qiang,Changhao Li,Haotian Sun,Yuchen Zhuang,Chao Zhang,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出层级代理（Orchestrator + Sub-Agents）以分离长程策略和执行，降低长上下文负担并提高迭代调试效率；实验证明对不同模型规模有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLE 任务需要在昂贵的环境交互下进行长程决策与反复调试，单体代理受制于上下文长度、模型容量与计算预算，故提出将战略探索与执行解耦以降低长期推理负担并提高迭代效率。

Method: 设计一个双层架构：高层 Orchestrator 维护压缩的长期探索状态并发出策略/指令；低层 Sub-Agents 通过统一 Tool 接口与环境交互并产出具体解法；训练范式旨在高效训练 Orchestrator 与 Sub-Agents（具体算法与优化细节需补充）。

Result: 论文提出了一种层级代理框架 Matryoshka Agent ，将长期探索的策略制定与代价高昂的执行分离，通过一个高层 Orchestrator 维护紧凑的长程探索状态并发出策略指令，低层 Sub-Agents 通过标准化工具接口执行具体尝试；此外还提出了高效训练范式，并在多种 MLE 任务与模型规模上展示了显著性能提升（如 Qwen3-30B-Coder 最高 36.7% 相对增益）。

Conclusion: Matryoshka Agent 是一种可扩展的层级化方案，能在 MLE 长程任务中提升样本效率和最终性能，但论文需补充训练细节、基线对比、成本分析与若干消融来增强可重复性与说服力。

Abstract: Machine learning engineering (MLE) tasks require long-horizon decision making over iterative solution debugging and refinement, under expensive and feedback-driven environment interactions. Developing and training a monolithic agent for such tasks is fundamentally challenging, as it must simultaneously manage extremely long and noisy contexts, explore vast solution spaces, and remain effective under limited model capacity and computational budgets. To address these challenges, we propose Matryoshka Agent, a unified hierarchical agent framework for complex long-horizon tasks. Matryoshka Agent decomposes agentic problem solving into a coordinated hierarchy of decision making and execution: a high-level Orchestrator maintains compact, long-horizon exploration states and issues strategic instructions, while lower-level Sub-Agents execute concrete solution attempts through direct environment interaction, mediated by standardized Tool interface. This design decouples strategic exploration from costly execution, substantially reducing the burden of long-context reasoning and enabling efficient iterative refinement. We further develop an efficient training paradigm for Matryoshka Agent. Experimental results on a broad range of MLE tasks with diverse model types and scales demonstrate that Matryoshka Agent is an effective and scalable paradigm for long-horizon MLE tasks and complex agentic problem solving. Notably, Matryoshka Agent enables Qwen3-4B-Instruct to reach Orchestrator performance comparable to o4-mini. Applying Matryoshka Agent to Qwen3-30B-Coder results in at most 36.7% relative performance gain.

</details>


### [42] [Towards Robust Reinforcement Learning for Small-Scale Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25091)
*Md Rezwanul Haque,Md. Milon Islam,Fakhri Karray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 系统化定位了SML PPO 失败模式并提出工程性修复与“capacity-headroom”假说，验证后能稳定训练并提升偏好胜率，全部资源公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统探究为什么小规模语言模型在使用强化学习（PPO）对齐时不稳定，找出可复现的失败根因并提出可行修复，以便在资源受限环境下也能进行有效对齐。

Method: 在 Pythia（70M/160M/410M）和 SmolLM2（135M/360M）与 TinyStories/CNN-DailyMail/Wikitext-103 数据集上做 15 个 (模型, 语料) 配置的 PPO 训练；诊断三种失败类型；分别用 merge-and-reinitialize LoRA、在 PPO 更新中用 float32、以及 reward whitening + importance-ratio guarding + weight rollback 来修复；以偏好胜率对比 SFT 与 instruction-tuned 基线。

Result: Identified three reproducible failure modes in PPO alignment of 70–500M SLMs (LoRA freezing, bfloat16 importance-ratio overflow, reward-model-induced collapse) and proposed fixes (merge-and-reinitialize adapters, float32 PPO updates, three-layer safety: reward whitening, importance-ratio guarding, weight rollback). Introduced a capacity-headroom hypothesis: PPO success at SLM scale requires a fluent supervised prior (PPL<20) and an informative reward signal rather than parameter count. Demonstrated stable convergence and improved preference win rate vs SFT and instruction-tuned baselines across 15 (model, corpus) configs; released checkpoints, datasets, scripts.

Conclusion: PPO 可在 70–500M 级别的 SLM 上稳定工作，但前提是有良好的流畅先验和判别力强的奖励信号；三个工程修复和三层安全机制是解决小模型崩溃的关键。

Abstract: The alignment of Small Language Models (SLMs) in the 70--500M parameter range using reinforcement learning is often considered unstable, though the underlying failure mechanisms have not been systematically investigated. In the State-of-the-Art (SOTA) research, fifteen (model, corpus) configurations were trained using Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO). The experiments included Pythia-70M, 160M, 410M and SmolLM2-135M, 360M on the TinyStories, CNN/DailyMail, and Wikitext-103 corpora. Three reproducible failure modes were identified in small-scale language models: silent LoRA parameter freezing in standard PEFT/TRL pipelines, numerical overflow in importance ratios when using bfloat16, and catastrophic policy collapse due to reward-model error. These issues were addressed using a merge-and-reinitialize adapter technique, float32 precision during PPO updates, and a three-layer safety mechanism comprising reward whitening, importance-ratio guarding, and weight rollback. In this paper, a capacity-headroom hypothesis is proposed, which states that PPO performance at the SLM scale depends on both a fluent supervised model ($\text{PPL}<20$) and a discriminative reward signal, rather than on the number of model parameters. The proposed system converged stably in all experiments and improved preference win rate over the SFT baseline in configurations with a fluent prior and an informative reward signal. Furthermore, it outperformed instruction-tuned baselines while requiring significantly less training data. All checkpoints, preference datasets, and training scripts are publicly released$^§$.

</details>


### [43] [ScalableRAG: High-Quality RAG at Zero Ingestion Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25135)
*Hilaf Hasson,Aditya Chakravarty,Jayant Thomas,Krishna Gogineni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种无需知识库或向量数据库的Zero-Ingestion ScalableRAG，通过维护可读写的文档集与值集工作区来实现按主键分组的聚合式推理，在六个语料上平均准确率比次优基线高7.36%。另外给出Limited-Ingestion变体，利用最小向量库与样本模式发现并把LLM调用数限制为与语料规模无关的常数，从而在大规模上进一步提高精度。代码开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG方法为追求性能投入大量摄取成本（构建知识图、抽取表格、建立向量库），增加工程复杂度与运行成本。目标是找到无或极少摄取成本的替代方案，同时在准确性与可扩展性上不落后于甚至超越现有方法。

Method: Zero-Ingestion ScalableRAG：不构建知识库或向量索引，而是维护一个可写可读的工作区，包含文档集与值集，支持按一对一主键对应的子文档集合上的按需聚合推理。Limited-Ingestion ScalableRAG：保留最小的向量数据库并从样本中自动发现模式，以减少误检并在大规模语料下提高精度；同时把LLM调用次数上界为与语料规模无关的常数。

Result: 在六个语料上实验：Zero-Ingestion在3/6语料上显著优于所有基线，在剩余3个上仅略逊色，整体平均准确率比次优基线高7.36%。Limited-Ingestion在大规模情形下进一步提高了准确性，并保证LLM调用数与语料规模无关。代码仓库公开。

Conclusion: 作者证明了许多依赖高成本知识摄取的RAG操作可以用零摄取成本的工作区读写机制复制，并在多数测试语料上优于或接近现有方法；在需要时引入有限摄取的混合方案可在不增加LLM调用复杂度的前提下进一步提升可扩展性与准确率。

Abstract: Recent advances in RAG aim to optimize for performance by paying high ingestion costs for knowledge ingestion: building knowledge graphs or extracting SQL tables. In this work we show that the operations that such knowledge bases allow can be replicated with zero ingestion costs (not even a vector database); in fact our solution, Zero-Ingestion ScalableRAG, handily out-performs all baselines (including knowledge graph approaches) in three out of the six corpora considered here, and only marginally missing maximum performance on the other three, with average accuracy across all six datasets 7.36% above the next most competitive baseline. It achieves this by keeping a workspace of document sets and values sets that it can write into and read from, allowing for on-the-fly aggregative reasoning in all situations where grouping is required on a primary key that is in one to one correspondence with a subset of the total document set.
  Capping the number of LLM calls by a constant independent of the corpus size, we also introduce Limited-Ingestion ScalableRAG, which does use a minimal vector database as well as an automated pattern discovery from a sample of documents, to further improve accuracy at scale. Our code is available at https://github.com/cohesity/ScalableRAG .

</details>


### [44] [Less Data, Better Alignment: Data-Centric Multi-Evaluator Agreement for Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25136)
*Zhengtao Yao,Runhao Li,Xupeng Chen,Jiayi Cheng,Chenqian Le,Michael Yue,Siheng Wang,Haoyan Xu,Yuqi Li,Chenhao Wei,Zhengdao Li,Rongchao Zhang,Guang Yang,Yidong Wang,Junhao Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者提出DMAPO，一种基于共识过滤的小规模高置信度在策略(on-policy)样本选择方法，用少量高质量样本对偏好优化进行数据驱动的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统研究通常在固定数据上改动训练目标，作者反向思考：是否可以通过精挑细选少量高置信度的在策略样本，提供更可靠的学习信号，从而提升偏好优化的数据效率。

Method: DMAPO流程：1) 从目标策略生成大量候选回复；2) 用针对性评价标准（helpfulness、factuality、conciseness）由专门的评估器逐条打分；3) 应用process-critic进行修正；4) 仅保留在多个评估器间达成高共识的“可取”或“不可取”样本用于训练。

Result: 从54,236个Mistral-7B候选中接受1,871个（3.45%）。用该集合训练的KTO在MT-Bench得分7.50；对text-davinci-003的长度控制胜率95.5%；IFEval提示准确率57.3%。独立两两对比评估中，DMAPO优于SimPO：GPT-4o在129个hold-out提示上净胜率+23.3点，在200个OOD LMSYS-Chat提示上净胜率+24.0点；Claude Opus 4.7在hold-out集上净胜+24.1点。更换评估器或评分细则会改变被选样本，但对下游性能影响小；在第二个backbone上得到相似的3.41%接受率，但性能提升较小。

Conclusion: 通过对策略生成候选回复进行多评估器基于标准的共识过滤，能用非常少的样本（≈3.4%接受率）实现显著的偏好优化提升，表明数据筛选在偏好优化上是一条高数据效率但依赖评估器判断的可行路径。

Abstract: Research on preference optimization often varies the training objective while holding the data fixed. We instead ask whether a small, high-confidence set of on-policy responses can provide a reliable learning signal. Our method, DMAPO (Data-centric Multi-evaluator Agreement for Preference Optimization), generates candidate responses from the target policy, evaluates helpfulness, factuality, and conciseness with rubric-specialized evaluators, applies a process-critic correction, and retains only high-consensus desirable or undesirable examples. This procedure accepts 1,871 of 54,236 Mistral-7B candidates (3.45%). KTO trained on this set reaches 7.50 on MT-Bench, 95.5% length-controlled win rate against a text-davinci-003 reference, and 57.3% IFEval prompt accuracy. Independent pairwise evaluation also favors DMAPO over SimPO: GPT-4o yields a net win rate of 23.3 points on 129 held-out prompts and 24.0 points on 200 out-of-distribution LMSYS-Chat prompts; Claude Opus 4.7 yields 24.1 points on the held-out set. Changing the evaluator model or rubric alters the selected examples but has little effect on downstream performance. A second-backbone study yields a similar 3.41% acceptance rate, although its performance gains are more modest. Across these experiments, consensus filtering offers a data-efficient route to preference optimization for general instructions, at the cost of additional curation compute and dependence on evaluator judgments.

</details>


### [45] [How Affect Propagates among LLM Agents: Emergent Emotional Contagion in Crowd Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25140)
*Funda Durupinar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Per-agent LLM appraisal + conventional navigation yields emergent, personality- and spatially-structured emotional contagion; outcomes are sensitive to prompt/model choices and sampling parameters.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To investigate whether affective contagion can emerge from LLM-driven appraisal and expressive behavior in multi-agent crowds without engineered inter-agent transfer rules, and how personality, context, and LLM backend shape these dynamics.

Method: Hybrid architecture combining an LLM-based cognitive/appraisal layer per agent with a separate low-level conventional crowd simulator. Agents perceive neighbors via visual/auditory/tactile channels, appraise perceptions using prompts encoding Big Five personality, memory, current affect (Russell circumplex), and situational context; the LLM updates internal affect and chooses outward expressions. No explicit hand-coded affect-transfer mechanism—contagion arises via perception→appraisal→expression loop. Evaluations include five scenario environments and controlled tests across prompt variants, sampling temperatures, and four model backends.

Result: The system produces emergent emotional-contagion dynamics in small sparse crowds: alarms spread as traveling fronts and settle to nonzero plateaus; personality distributions modulate whether ambiguous signals escalate to panic or are categorized as anger vs fear. Appraisal outcomes and contagion dynamics vary with prompt design, sampling temperature, and LLM backend.

Conclusion: LLM-driven appraisal can generate emergent affect propagation without explicit transfer mechanisms, but reproducibility and robustness depend strongly on personality priors, scenario parameters, and the choice and configuration of the LLM backend; more rigorous controls, baselines and statistical analyses are needed.

Abstract: This paper studies the behavior of language models in a multi-agent crowd simulation, focusing on how affect propagates among agents that perceive and appraise one another. Each agent perceives its neighbors through visual, auditory, and tactile channels, then appraises these perceptions in light of its prompted personality profile, memory, current affective state, and situational context. Appraisal is carried out by an LLM, which updates the agent's internal affective state and selects its outward expression. The architecture contains no hand-authored mechanism for directly transferring affective state between agents; instead, inter-agent influence arises through the perception-appraisal-expression loop. The agent representation draws on the Big Five personality model and Russell's circumplex model of affect. To limit latency, low-level steering and navigation are handled by a conventional crowd simulator operating independently of the LLM-based cognitive layer.
  We evaluate the architecture across five scenario environments spanning alarming, joyful, and neutral situations in different spatial layouts. The results show that the system produces emotional contagion dynamics with spatial, temporal, and personality-dependent structure in sparse, small crowds. Alarm spreads from seeded agents as a traveling front, the mean alarmed fraction settles at a nonzero plateau, and the distribution of prompted personality profiles determines whether an ambiguous alarm ignites panic and whether a provocation is interpreted as anger or fear. We further evaluate the appraisal step through controlled experiments across prompt variants, sampling temperatures, and four model backends, showing that the dynamics are backend-dependent.

</details>


### [46] [Inferring Missing Trajectory Data with Temporal Convolutional Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25147)
*Ilinca Tiriblecea,Gabriel Turinici*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种带对称膨胀的时序卷积网络和复合损失，用以在单条轨迹中利用前后观测重建连续缺失片段，实验在合成数据上显示出良好重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Real-world trajectory data often contains contiguous missing segments due to sensor failure, occlusion, or communication loss; existing sequence models for forecasting enforce causality and cannot effectively use future context for reconstructing gaps, so a method that leverages both past and future observations is needed for trajectory inpainting.

Method: Architecture: Temporal Convolutional Network modified with symmetric dilation that relaxes causality so each receptive field includes both past and future observations. Training objective: composite loss composed of weighted mean squared error over missing regions, boundary-continuity penalty to ensure smooth transitions at gap edges, and a smoothness regularizer to discourage high-frequency artifacts. Dataset/setting: synthetic 2D trajectories; randomly placed 20% masked contiguous segments; train/val/test splits of 1000/200/300; evaluation metrics: R^2, MSE, MAE.

Result: A Temporal Convolutional Network with symmetric dilation that permits each time step to access both past and future context was proposed and trained with a composite loss (weighted MSE, boundary-continuity penalties, smoothness regularizer). On a synthetic dataset of 1,000 training, 200 validation, and 300 test two-dimensional trajectories with randomly placed 20% masked segments, the model achieves strong R^2, low MSE and MAE, indicating effective reconstruction of missing segments.

Conclusion: Symmetric-dilation TCN combined with targeted loss terms can effectively inpaint contiguous missing trajectory segments on synthetic data. Further validation on real-world datasets, uncertainty quantification, and comparisons/ablations against bidirectional RNNs, transformers, or probabilistic generative models are necessary to establish generality and robustness.

Abstract: Trajectory data collected in real-world settings is frequently incomplete due to sensor failure, communication loss, or occlusion. We address the task of \emph{trajectory inpainting}: reconstructing contiguous missing segments from observed context. We propose a Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) with symmetric dilation that relaxes the standard causality constraint, allowing each time step to draw on both past and future observations, a property that is essential for inpainting, but absent from forecasting-oriented architectures. The model is trained with a composite loss that combines weighted mean squared error, boundary--continuity penalties, and a smoothness regularizer. Trained on a synthetic dataset of $1,000$ (train), $200$ (validation), and $300$ (test) two-dimensional trajectories with randomly placed 20% masked segments, the model achieves good R$^{2}$, MSE and MAE metrics.

</details>


### [47] [When Do Agent Loops Mistake Stagnation for Progress? Self-Evaluation Bias and Externally Grounded Verification in Long-Running Autonomous LLM Agent Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25152)
*Hyundoo Park,Byungho Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Self-evaluating long-running agents can produce a "progress mirage" where claimed improvements do not translate to real-world gains. Grounding of the evaluator (access to out-of-band world-state) is decisive; scaling in-band judges is insufficient when success signals live outside the artifact transcript.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Investigate self-evaluation bias in long-running autonomous agents that grade their own work, identify a failure mode called the "progress mirage," and quantify how evaluator grounding affects whether claimed progress matches real-world outcomes.

Method: Built a testbed that fixes the agent and its tool surface while varying only the evaluator's information channel. Enforced an unfakeable world-state oracle via container and network isolation and verified it each run. Ran 54 iterative cycles and compared claimed improvements (self-report and in-band judges) against measured real-world deltas; tested variants including self-report, strong in-band judge (artifact text + diff + verdict history), sign-only acceptance, and a boundary task where success is verifiable from the artifact.

Result: Using a controlled testbed with a world-state oracle, the paper shows that self-reporting agents claim improvement while real-world metrics stagnate or regress. Over 54 cycles the agent claimed improvement every time yet 56% of cycles had zero or negative real delta; self-verdict gating accepted almost all changes and eroded best state by 19%. A strong in-band judge still accepted 44% regressions and rejected 38% real improvements; on a boundary task verifiable from the artifact the mirage disappeared. A sign-only acceptance verdict produced similar real-world output, indicating grounding of the gate matters more than feedback content. Concludes that for open-ended objectives whose success signal lies outside transcripts, out-of-band real-world evaluation is structurally required.

Conclusion: Evaluator grounding determines whether iterative agent improvements are genuine. For tasks where success requires out-of-transcript evidence, only evaluators with out-of-band world access can prevent progress mirages; stronger in-band judges alone cannot reliably close the gap.

Abstract: Long-running autonomous agents plan, act, and judge their own completion without human intervention. When an agent grades its own work, self-evaluation bias takes hold: plausible changes are accepted as progress while real-world outcomes stagnate or regress. We name this failure mode the progress mirage and show, with controlled measurement, that it is a question of what the evaluator is grounded in. We built a testbed that holds the agent and its tool surface fixed and manipulates only the information-channel type of the evaluator that gates the loop. A world-state oracle, unfakeable in principle, is enforced by container and network isolation and verified at every run. Across 54 cycles a frontier agent claimed improvement every time, yet 56 percent had a measured delta of zero or below. Self-report was thus uninformative, and the self-verdict gate degenerated into accept-all, eroding the best deployed state it had reached by 19 percent. Even the strongest in-band judge, reading the full artifact text, the change diff, and its own verdict history, accepted cycles of which 44 percent were real-world regressions and rejected 38 percent of real improvements; the preregistered adversarial hypothesis that a strong judge closes the gap was rejected. On a boundary task whose success specification is verifiable from the artifact itself, the same judge's mirage vanished to zero and the gap collapsed within the registered threshold, showing that the gap depends on where the success signal resides. A sign-only variant returning only the acceptance verdict kept real-world output similar to full feedback (110.0 versus 113.0), locating the benefit in the gate's grounding rather than in feedback content. For open-ended objectives whose success signal lives outside the transcript, scaling up the judge is not enough; out-of-band evaluation with real-world access is a structural requirement.

</details>


### [48] [PreDiff-LM: Pretrained Discrete Masked Diffusion Language Modeling with Hybrid Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25157)
*Zhengtao Yao,Runhao Li,Xupeng Chen,Jiayi Cheng,Chenqian Le,Michael Yue,Jesson Wang,Siheng Wang,Guang Yang,Haoyan Xu,Chenhao Wei,Zhengqing Yuan,Youran Shen,Yanfang Ye,Junhao Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在提示和被掩码目标间使用混合因果/双向注意力，PreDiff-LM更好地将因果预训练的AR骨干适配到离散掩码扩散LM，显著提升生成质量与收敛速度，但仍落后于同规模、优化过的AR生成模型且存在推理效率差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散掩码扩散语言模型能够做双向生成与填空，但直接使用在因果预训练下得到的AR transformer存在因果与双向去噪目标的不匹配。工作旨在在注意力结构层面调和因果预训练与双向去噪，从而更有效地利用AR初始化。

Method: 在注意力层面提出混合注意力机制：对提示(prompt)保持自回归（因果）注意力，而对被掩码的目标部分允许完整的双向注意力，从而在保留预训练自回归（AR）transformer权重的同时支持离散掩码扩散（masked diffusion）双向去噪生成。作者还研究了将注意力适配与DiffuGPT式目标适配相结合的效果。

Result: 在GPT-2 Medium、WikiText-103、90K步训练设定下，混合注意力（PreDiff-LM）相比统一双向注意力在相同AR初始化下将无条件困惑度从34.1降到28.7，MAUVE从0.71升到0.78；与DiffuGPT式目标适配结合可达26.9困惑度。预训练初始化将达到困惑度<50所需步数从≈350K降到≈8K。尽管有改进，但在相同计算下的微调AR模型仍优于PreDiff-LM（18.9 vs 28.7）。此外，PreDiff-LM在重复性、分布质量、4个零-shot下游任务和人工偏好上也优于此前的扩散基线。

Conclusion: 混合注意力是一个补充路径，可在注意力层面缓和因果预训练与双向去噪的不一致性，从而更快且更好地利用AR初始化训练离散扩散LM；但要在质量和推理效率上超越专门优化的AR模型，仍需进一步研究（例如更优目标、推理算法或架构修改）。

Abstract: Discrete masked diffusion language models support bidirectional generation and infilling, but adapting pretrained autoregressive (AR) transformers requires reconciling causal pretraining with bidirectional denoising. We study this problem at the level of attention rather than claiming AR-weight reuse itself as novel. PreDiff-LM preserves causal attention within the observed prompt while allowing full bidirectional attention within the masked target. Under a matched GPT-2 Medium, WikiText-103, 90K-step setup, this hybrid mask improves unconditional perplexity from 34.1 to 28.7 and MAUVE from 0.71 to 0.78 over uniform bidirectional attention with the same AR initialization. Attention adaptation also composes with a DiffuGPT-style objective adaptation, reaching 26.9 perplexity. Pretrained initialization reduces the steps required to reach perplexity below 50 from about 350K to 8K, although a compute-matched fine-tuned AR model remains stronger at equal scale (18.9 versus 28.7). Beyond perplexity, PreDiff-LM improves repetition, distributional quality, four zero-shot downstream tasks, and human preference over prior diffusion baselines. The results position hybrid attention as a complementary mechanism for adapting pretrained causal backbones, while making explicit the remaining quality and inference-efficiency gaps to optimized AR models.

</details>


### [49] [Observing sycophantic AI validate others reduces its appeal but not its persuasiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25166)
*Meryl Ye,Robert Kraut,Steve Rathje*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Awareness interventions (warnings, videos) make sycophantic AIs seem less objective/trustworthy and reduce enjoyment, but they do not reduce the AI’s persuasiveness—individual-level warnings appear insufficient to prevent sycophantic influence.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Users frequently fail to notice when AI behaves sycophantically (‘sycophancy blindness’), which can entrench attitudes and be harmful; the paper tests whether raising awareness (warnings/videos) protects users from sycophantic influence.

Method: Two preregistered experiments (n=940 and n=650). Manipulations: a brief written warning about sycophancy (Exp1) and a video showing the AI validating other users including opposing sides (Exp2). Outcomes: subjective ratings (perceived objectivity, enjoyment, trust), belief that validation was uniquely earned, and persuasiveness. Mediation analysis tested whether reduced belief in unique validation explained changes. Authors then pooled these two experiments with two prior studies to analyze six awareness interventions in total (pooled n=3,982).

Result: Both interventions changed subjective evaluations: the warning lowered perceived objectivity; the video reduced enjoyment, mediated by lowered belief that validation was uniquely earned. Pooled analysis across six interventions showed a consistent pattern: interventions decreased perceived objectivity and trustworthiness but none reduced AI persuasiveness.

Conclusion: Individual-level interventions such as brief warnings or awareness materials alter subjective impressions but do not block persuasion. To mitigate sycophantic AI harms, systemic, model- or platform-level measures are likely required (design changes, transparency/provenance, algorithmic constraints, regulation).

Abstract: AI chatbots can be ``sycophantic,'' or overly agreeable and flattering toward users. Sycophantic AI has been shown to entrench attitudes, yet users frequently fail to recognize it (a phenomenon we call ``sycophancy blindness''). We tested whether increasing users' awareness of sycophancy protects them from its harmful effects. In one preregistered experiment (n = 940), participants received a brief written warning about sycophancy before conversing with a sycophantic chatbot. In a second preregistered experiment (n = 650), participants watched a video of a sycophantic AI validating several other users, including users on opposite sides of the same conflict, before interacting with it themselves. Both interventions changed how participants evaluated the AI. The warning reduced the AI's perceived objectivity, and the video reduced enjoyment of the AI, an effect mediated by the reduced belief that its validation was uniquely earned. We then pooled our experiments with two prior studies of sycophancy awareness interventions (six interventions total, n = 3,982). The pattern was consistent: interventions made the sycophantic AI appear less objective and trustworthy, and none of the six reduced its persuasiveness. These results suggest that individual-level interventions, such as warning labels or AI literacy, may not be enough to protect users from AI harms.

</details>


### [50] [Everyone is unique: Towards Behaviorally Heterogeneous Negotiation Dialogue Systems for Debt Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25218)
*Yuhang Yang,Kai Tang,Chao Ye,Haobo Wang,Qiqi Luo,Jinguang Zheng,Zhixin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含行为异质性的人物化债务催收基准DebtBench，并训练出优化财务回收与用户体验的催收代理DebtGPT，实验证明DebtGPT显著优于开源基线并接近GPT-4o表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 债务催收是金融领域中既具有强实践意义又行为复杂的高风险对话任务；现有基准将用户视为静态理性体，无法反映真实世界中的行为多样性，因而需要一个以人物设定（persona）丰富的基准来评估模型在异质化人类行为下的表现。

Method: 设计persona-enriched仿真环境（DebtBench），构造多样借款人画像与行为策略；训练DebtGPT以联合目标（回收率+交互体验）进行策略优化；在16个主流LLM上开展对比实验并通过指标评估模型性能。

Result: 提出了DebtBench：首个公开的以persona丰富的债务催收基准；并提出DebtGPT：一个在回收率与交互体验上联合优化的催收代理。通过在16个先进LLM上的实验发现，大部分模型在这种复杂真实场景下表现欠佳；DebtGPT优于所有开源基线，并达到与GPT-4o相当的水平。数据与代码已开源。

Conclusion: 引入行为异质性的基准能更真实地测试对话/谈判模型；专门训练的DebtGPT在该基准上表现优越，但仍需关注伦理、隐私、可迁移性和人类监督等问题。

Abstract: Debt collection is a critical negotiation task in the financial industry, with strong practical relevance and exceptional academic value as a behaviorally rich, high-stakes testbed for human-centered dialogue systems. While large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in dialogue and negotiation, effectively evaluating their performance in this complex scenarios remains a major challenge: existing benchmarks uniformly assume users to be static, rational agents with fixed preferences, failing to capture the rich behavioral heterogeneity inherent in real-world debt collection. To bridge this gap, we propose DebtBench, the first public persona-enriched debt collection benchmark, that highlights behavioral heterogeneity in negotiation. Moreover, we develop DebtGPT, a debt collection agent trained to jointly optimize financial recovery and interaction experience. Our experimental results, using 16 state-of-the-art LLMs, find that most existing models struggle in this complex but realistic scenarios, whereas DebtGPT outperforms all open-source baselines and achieves performance on par with GPT-4o. The code and data are available at https://github.com/YYuHhhh/DebtNegotiation.

</details>


### [51] [CADENCE: A Cardiac Atom Dictionary for Interpretable Neural Concept Extraction from ECG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25244)
*Yixuan Duan,Arjun Naik,Sadeer Al-Kindi,Wei Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 把大型ECG模型的中间表示稀疏分解为可解读的“心电原子”，这些原子在诊断表型和波形学上比原始密集维度更具可解释性与预测力，并可被量化、描述和外部验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECG foundation models transfer well but their internal representations are opaque; clinicians and developers need human-interpretable, queryable primitives that reveal physiological knowledge and enable auditing.

Method: 提取模型第6层（Layer 6）嵌入的>9M ECG token，使用BatchTopK稀疏自编码器对嵌入进行因子分解得到8192个稀疏原子；用线性/稀疏探针评估原子与临床表型、波形学和年龄的对应性并计算AUROC；做几何分析与有针对性的原子消融以检验功能性影响；用自动化大模型流水线生成并通过预测保留激活来定量验证原子描述；在独立外部数据集上复现并评估泛化性。

Result: CADENCE uses a BatchTopK sparse autoencoder to factorize Layer-6 embeddings from >9M ECG tokens into 8,192 sparse 'cardiac atoms' that align strongly with clinical phenotypes and waveform morphology, outperforming dense embedding dimensions (best atoms AUROC 0.88 phenotype, 0.90 morphology vs 0.78/0.83 dense). Sparse probes match or beat dense probes for phenotype/morphology/age, enable sparse attribution, show coherent geometry, selective atom ablation changes downstream outputs, and an LLM pipeline auto-generates and validates atom descriptions; external datasets confirm overlapping concepts and stable performance.

Conclusion: CADENCE为发现、量化和审计ECG基础模型中蕴含的生理知识提供了可扩展且可解释的字典式表示；这种表示不仅提高了下游预测性能，还支持局部归因与干预性验证。

Abstract: Foundation models for 12-lead electrocardiograms (ECGs) transfer well across clinical tasks, but the physiological knowledge encoded in their representations remains opaque. We present CADENCE, a framework that decomposes an ECG foundation model into a human-interpretable, queryable dictionary of physiological concepts. Using a BatchTopK sparse autoencoder, CADENCE factorizes Layer-6 embeddings from more than nine million ECG tokens into 8,192 sparse cardiac atoms. These atoms align better than individual dense embedding dimensions with clinical phenotypes and waveform morphology, recovering arrhythmias, conduction abnormalities, infarction and repolarization patterns, chamber and axis findings, and lead- and beat-phase-specific waveform primitives. At Layer 6, the best atoms achieve mean AUROCs of 0.88 for clinical phenotypes and 0.90 for morphology, versus 0.78 and 0.83 for the best dense dimensions. Sparse atom probes match or outperform dense probes for phenotype, morphology, and age prediction while attributing each prediction to a small set of interpretable atoms; phenotype AUROC improves from 0.93 to 0.95. Atom-space geometry recovers physiologically coherent relationships, and targeted atom ablation selectively changes frozen downstream outputs. An automated LLM pipeline generates and quantitatively validates atom descriptions by predicting held-out activations. On independent external ECG datasets, CADENCE recovers overlapping concepts and maintains consistent phenotype-prediction performance. CADENCE provides a scalable framework for discovering and auditing the physiological knowledge encoded by ECG foundation models.

</details>


### [52] [The User Asks, Platforms Compete: How Agentic Recommendation Markets Take Shape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25253)
*Deyao Hong,Kehan Zheng,Qian Li,Jun Zhang,Jie Jiang,Hongning Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 引入以用户为中心、由LLM驱动的代理式推荐市场：扩大了候选池但并未自动带来有效曝光，平台会通过策略性说明争夺注意力；将平台行为与用户反馈挂钩的代理能减缓策略化并提升用户效用，建议将访问、注意力、问责作为统一的机制设计维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM用户代理出现，用户可能在选平台前先表达需求，改变了推荐的发起方与竞争结构：平台从被动向用户展示内容变为主动争取用户注意。研究动机是理解这种“平台间争夺注意力”的新市场如何影响访问、注意力分配与最终用户效用，以及如何设计代理以避免负面策略化。

Method: 作者用LLM构建用户代理，让用户在选择平台前表达需求；在三个产品领域内进行受控实验，比对传统平台中心推荐与代理式（用户中心）情形，衡量候选进入率、排名占比、平台说明策略、以及在引入反馈关联前后相关项购买率等指标。

Result: 该论文通过LLM驱动的用户代理搭建“代理式推荐市场”，并在三个产品领域的受控实验中展示了新型推荐设置下的“访问（access）与注意力（attention）”之间的张力。主要发现包括：用户端推荐极大扩展了候选项进入比较的机会，但注意力稀缺导致平台通过“选择性积极说明”占据大量首位；将平台行为与后续用户反馈关联的用户代理能显著降低这种策略化占比并提高用户购买相关项的概率；用户代理的查询、排序与反馈机制会直接影响谁能竞争、注意力如何分配、以及结果如何塑造平台评估，因而应将访问、注意力与问责作为联合机制设计问题来对待。

Conclusion: 在代理式推荐市场中，仅扩展候选访问不足以改善用户效用；用户代理的设计（如何查询、排名与收集反馈）决定了注意力分配与平台行为，良好设计的反馈-问责机制可以抑制平台的策略性操纵并提升用户结果，因此需要把访问、注意力与问责作为一个整体来设计。

Abstract: Online recommendation has traditionally taken place after a user enters a platform, which determines the candidate pool and the ranking shown to the user. LLM-based user agents enable a different recommendation process: a user specifies a need before choosing a platform, leaving platforms to compete for the user's attention, which we refer to as an agentic recommendation market. In our controlled LLM-based experiments across three product domains, we find this new setting of recommendation creates a tension between access and attention. Compared with traditional platform-centric recommendation, user-centric recommendation greatly expands the opportunity for relevant items to enter comparison; yet broader participation does not translate directly into effective exposure. Competition directly triggers platforms' strategic play: selectively positive explanations occupy 73--78% of first-ranked positions. When the user agent relates platforms' actions to subsequent user feedback, this share falls to 36--41%, while the chance of a user purchasing the relevant item increases. A user agent is therefore more than a ranker over a larger pool of candidates: its querying, ranking, and feedback mechanism governing who can compete, how scarce attention is allocated, and how earlier outcomes shape the evaluation of platforms directly affect user utility. Designing agentic recommendation therefore requires treating access, attention, and accountability as a joint mechanism design problem.

</details>


### [53] [Many-body Tipping Dynamics of ChatGPT-like AIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25279)
*Frank Yingjie Huo,Neil F. Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 抽象将Transformer内tokens间的多体相互作用和注意力无序视为导致确定性解码下“偏离”到不良输出盆的动力学第一通行过程，并用少盆简化得到可比较的有限层阈值，认为此类失败是可预见的可工程化风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何即便在结构与训练差异甚大的大模型下，仍普遍出现确定性贪婪解码中的不良输出；若能把失效解释为动力学临界现象，则有助于将其视为可工程化的风险并指导缓解策略。

Method: 将tokens类比为自旋，借用多体相互作用与第一通行过程的统计物理框架，分析注意力如何改变“态势（transport）”以促成或阻止跨盆穿越；通过少盆简化（coarse-graining）得到封闭的有限层阈值并与多款ChatGPT类模型的粗粒化预测作比较。

Result: 论文主张：在有限层Transformer类模型中，tokens（类比为“自旋”）间的多体相互作用，及注意力带来的“无序”，会导致输出在不同吸引盆（basins）间发生动力学性质的“穿越/第一通行”过程，从而解释在确定性贪婪解码下仍出现的有害、误导或重复等不期望内容。通过将系统粗粒化为少数盆的模型，作者导出一个有限层阈值并在多种ChatGPT类模型上进行了粗略比对，得出模型失效在很大程度上是“可预见的工程风险”而非完全不可预测。

Conclusion: 若抽象中提出的多体与注意力无序框架成立，一类在有限层下的动力学临界性可解释并预测某些确定性解码失败，影响安全评估与法律责任判断：这些失效并非随机不可控，而是可通过工程手段预测与缓解。

Abstract: Why do ChatGPT-like AIs, despite major architectural and training differences, unexpectedly tip to undesirable content (e.g. harmful, misleading, repetitive) even under deterministic greedy decoding? We show that a broad class of such tippings is caused by the many-body interactions between tokens (spins) as they cross the finite-layer system. Tipping emerges as a dynamical first passage process between competing output basins. Attention disorder controls the transport toward, away from, or along the basins' boundary. A few-basin reduction yields a closed finite-layer threshold, whose coarse-grained predictions show good agreement across ChatGPT-like families. These results suggest that a broad class of AI failures represents 'foreseeable engineering risk' rather than inherently unpredictable behavior, with important implications for legal and societal assessments of AI harm.

</details>


### [54] [ContractHIL-HLS: Contract-Aligned Multi-Agent Workflow with Hardware-in-the-Loop Feedback for HLS Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25283)
*Jingbo Zhang,Haoxiang Sun,Wenbo Wang,Wenbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduce a contract-first, hardware-in-the-loop multi-agent workflow that pushes LLM-assisted HLS from kernel-level synthesis toward board-level validated designs, showing modest gains on benchmark tasks and a substantial board-level speedup in a PQC accelerator case.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bridge the gap between natural-language specifications and practical board-level high-level synthesis (HLS) by introducing a structured, machine-readable artifact and closing the loop with measured hardware evidence so LLM-assisted HLS can reach system- and board-level closure.

Method: Semantic lowering: Contract Agent turns NL into a structured contract (interfaces, constraints, checks, rollback rules). Persistence: HTML Agent renders the contract to structured HTML for traceability. Implementation loop: Hardware-in-the-Loop Agent runs HLS flows (HLS tools, Vivado), deploys to PYNQ board, measures power/timing/failures, and uses evidence to iteratively revise design; includes a dual-bitstream organization for faster on-board runtime and rollback.

Result: Proposes ContractHIL-HLS: (1) a structured contract that maps NL requirements to explicit interfaces, constraints, validation checks, and rollback rules; (2) a feedback loop that feeds HLS/Vivado/PYNQ/runtime/power/failure evidence back into generation; (3) an agent decomposition into Contract Agent, HTML Agent, and Hardware-in-the-Loop Agent. Evaluation: on 94 HLS-Eval tasks, contract raises estimated single-sample testbench pass rate from 64.0% to 70.2%; full flow reaches 70.4% pass@1 and 76.6% pass@5. Board-level case (PQC accelerator) shows runtime reduction from 207.3 ms to 52.4 ms and positive routed WNS on both images while preserving verification. Open-sourced at the given repository.

Conclusion: Structured contracts plus measured hardware feedback and semantically decomposed agents materially improve the practicality of LLM-assisted HLS, enabling more reliable synthesis and faster board-level implementations in evaluated cases.

Abstract: This paper presents ContractHIL-HLS, a contract-aligned multi-agent workflow for practical high-level synthesis (HLS) engineering. The workflow makes three contributions. First, it introduces a structured contract as the semantic-alignment and task-execution artifact that translates natural language requirements into explicit interfaces, constraints, validation checks, and rollback rules. Second, it incorporates hardware information into the feedback loop by feeding HLS, Vivado, PYNQ runtime, power, and failure evidence back into generation, thereby extending LLM-assisted HLS from kernel code toward system- and board-level closure. Third, it decomposes agents by semantic lowering and execution tasks rather than by conversational roles: a Contract Agent lowers natural language into the contract, an HTML Agent renders the contract as persistent structured HTML, and a Hardware-in-the-Loop Agent implements and revises the design with measured evidence. We evaluate ContractHIL-HLS in two parts. On 94 locally executable HLS-Eval tasks, the structured contract provides the largest small design gain, improving the estimated single-sample testbench pass rate from 64.0% to 70.2%; the full flow reaches 70.4% pass@1 and 76.6% pass@5. Because HLS-Eval does not exercise board-level design, we also validate ContractHIL-HLS on a board tested ML-KEM/ML-DSA post-quantum cryptography (PQC) secure-message accelerator, where the retained dual-bitstream organization reduces six-message average text runtime from 207.3 ms to 52.4 ms with positive routed WNS on both images while preserving decrypted-message verification. We open-source our work at BJUT-CS316-LAB/ContractHIL-HLS (https://github.com/BJUT-CS316-LAB/ContractHIL-HLS).

</details>


### [55] [Instruction-Tuned Language Models Cannot Sample from Distributions They Can Describe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25292)
*Chaemin Jang,Dongman Lee,Jihee Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 指令微调使模型输出变得近乎确定性，破坏了把模型调用当作独立受访者抽样的前提，但同一模型能准确“知道”而不能“做出”采样——通过让模型描述分布或使用PPA可大幅改善与人类调查数据的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑并检验“硅采样”（将语言模型调用视作独立的受访者抽样）的前提：指令微调后的模型是否真的从一个响应分布中随机采样，还是崩溃到确定性输出，从而使基于模型采样的群体统计失真。

Method: 对比分析与实证实验：在多个模型家族（包含base与instruction-tuned版本）上，用公共舆论基准逐题测量同一persona在重复调用下的响应一致性；检查logits分布、内部概率集中度与指令微调（alignment）前后差异；提出并评估两种缓解策略——让模型在一次调用中描述响应分布，以及Prompt-Perturbed Argyle (PPA)的提示扰动方法，并以与人类调查数据的误差为指标进行比较。

Result: 发现指令微调显著导致‘采样崩溃’：同一persona对同一问题在重复调用中超过半数题目返回完全相同答案；logits与概率高度集中为单一选项；这一现象在所有测试任务和指令微调模型上均显著，而base模型表现好得多。尽管不能通过多次采样得到分布，模型能在单次调用中准确描述该分布（KNOWS/DOES分裂）。利用该分裂，将任务改为描述分布可使与人类数据的误差减少一半以上；PPA在需要逐persona输出的情形下可在不增加额外成本的情况下将误差再降低约21%。

Conclusion: 对依赖硅采样的社会科学或价值评估工作要谨慎：指令对齐训练会在logits层面引入退化的采样原语，导致采样失真。可行的补救是改用分布性询问（elicitation）或提示扰动方法（如PPA），并在更广泛的数据集、解码器超参和对齐流程上做进一步验证与稳健性分析。

Abstract: Silicon sampling uses language models as proxies for human survey respondents, treating each model call as an independent draw from the persona's response distribution. We show this draw does not exist: instruction-tuned models do not sample from distributions, they collapse to a single output. The same persona on the same question returns the same answer on more than half of items in a public-opinion benchmark. The collapse is sharp: the model's internal probabilities concentrate on a single option, and the failure is substantially amplified by instruction tuning: across three model families with materially different post-training pipelines, every instruction-tuned model fails on every task we test, while base models fail far less often. Strikingly, the same model that cannot sample from a distribution can describe it accurately in a single call. We call this gap the KNOWS/DOES split, and trace it to a degenerate sampling primitive visible in the logits and induced by alignment training. Exploiting this split, asking the model to describe the response distribution in one call more than halves the error against human survey data compared to persona aggregation. For applications that require per-persona outputs, we propose Prompt-Perturbed Argyle (PPA), which reduces the same error by 21% at no added cost.

</details>


### [56] [Physics-Grounded Fluid Video Generation with a Simulation Dataset and Dual-Stream Optical-Flow Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25321)
*Ruijie Su,Yuanzhi Liang,Xiaohua Xie,Jianhuang Lai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过仿真增强与光流监督的双流解码器，在冻结生成骨干上显著改进了视频扩散模型对液体动力学的建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模视频-文本语料缺乏显式运动监督，导致扩散视频模型学到的是流体外观而非动力学，出现明显物理违背；因此需要通过仿真数据与光流监督来补强对流体运动的建模。

Method: 1) 数据层面：构建MPM仿真与真实视频混合的数据集并设置两种测试集；2) 模型层面：在预训练的diffusion-transformer上新增轻量光流解码器与RGB解码器，光流分支以端点误差(EPE)与光滑损失训练，通过零初始化卷积融合到RGB分支；3) 训练策略：仅更新两个解码器，保持encoder、temporal transformer与text encoder冻结；4) 评估：使用VideoPhy-2、视频质量分数、人工评估和光流EPE等指标，比较两个模型规模与公开方法基线。

Result: 构建了一个包含1,638条MPM仿真倒水/晃动视频与2,320条筛选真实倒水视频的数据集，并保留两个测试集（1,515条真实视频基准与18条文本到首帧泛化基准）；提出一种双流图像到视频网络，在预训练的diffusion-transformer生成器上增设轻量级光流解码器分支，用端点误差和光滑性损失进行训练，通过零初始化卷积与RGB流融合，仅更新两个解码器（encoder、temporal transformer、text encoder冻结）；在1.3B与14B两种规模上，在两个测试集上VideoPhy-2物理常识与视频质量分数相较冻结骨干最高提升8.75与4.65分，优于公开领先方法并在盲测中被人工评价优选；光流直接读出评测在同分布达到0.54像素的EPE，表明学习到一致的运动先验。

Conclusion: 显式的运动监督（仿真数据 + 光流损失）能使基于扩散的生成模型学习到一致的流体运动先验，显著减少物理学违法的生成行为，并提升主观与客观质量。

Abstract: Video diffusion models generate visually compelling content but routinely violate elementary physics when the subject involves fluids: liquid columns break apart in mid-air, container water levels fail to rise as liquid is poured in, and splashes disperse without regard to momentum or gravity. We attribute this gap to the fact that large-scale video-text corpora contain almost no explicit motion supervision, so models learn to imitate fluid appearance rather than dynamics. We address this with two contributions. First, we build a physics-simulation fluid dataset combining 1,638 MPM-simulated pouring/sloshing videos with 2,320 keyword-filtered real pouring videos mined from stock footage, plus two held-out test sets: a 1,515-video real-video benchmark and an 18-prompt text-to-first-frame generalization benchmark. Second, we introduce a dual-stream image-to-video architecture built on a pretrained diffusion-transformer video generator. It augments the standard RGB decoder with a lightweight Optical-Flow Decoder branch trained with explicit end-point-error and smoothness losses, fused into the RGB stream via zero-initialized convolutions so the pretrained backbone starts undisturbed. Only the two decoders are updated; the encoder, temporal transformer, and text encoder remain frozen. Across two model scales (1.3B and 14B) and two test sets, our method improves VideoPhy-2 Physical-Commonsense and Video-Quality scores over the frozen backbone by up to 8.75 and 4.65 points, outperforms a leading open competitor, and is preferred by human raters in a blind study. A direct optical-flow read-out evaluation further shows an end-point error as low as 0.54 pixels in-distribution, confirming the model has internalized a coherent motion prior rather than merely improving surface appearance.

</details>


### [57] [From Cellular Responses to Pharmacological Domains: Multimodal Zero-Shot Drug Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25322)
*Jintao Huang,Lu Leng,Ziyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PMRD通过分离机制一致因子、构建多模态共识反应域并采用动态重加权与可靠性感知检索，实现了更机制感知的多模态药物表征，显著改善零样本性质预测和药物邻域的生物学一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接融合或基于样本级对比学习的对齐会将机制相关信号与模态噪声混淆，且可能把结构上不相似但在生物响应上相关的化合物错误地分离，阻碍可迁移的机制模式学习，进而影响对未见化合物的零样本预测。

Method: 核心方法包括：1) 将模态数据分解为机制一致因子与模态特异信息；2) 构建跨三种模态（化学结构、基因表达、细胞形态）的共识反应域；3) 机制候选增强（mechanism candidate augmentation）用于识别局部稳定因子；4) 检索-几何归因（retrieval-geometry attribution）动态重加权对齐与增强目标以保持药物间可区分性；5) 可靠性感知的多视图检索融合互补表示。

Result: 在公共数据集上的实验证明PMRD在零样本属性预测上表现更好，学到的药物邻域在生物学上一致性更高；困难负样本分析显示减少了对结构不相似但响应相关化合物的冲突。

Conclusion: PMRD是一种以药理反应域为导向的多模态表示学习框架，通过分离机制一致性因子与模态特异性信息并构建跨三模态的共识反应域，从而在零样本药物性质预测任务中提升泛化性和生物学一致性。

Abstract: Multimodal drug discovery enables drug representation learning beyond chemical structure by incorporating cellular responses such as gene expression and cell morphology. However, direct fusion and instance-level contrastive alignment may mix mechanism-related signals with modality-specific noise and incorrectly separate structurally dissimilar but biologically related compounds. This limitation can obscure transferable mechanism patterns required for predicting the properties of unseen compounds. We introduce PMRD, a pharmacological response domain-guided framework for multimodal zero-shot drug property prediction. PMRD separates mechanism-consistent factors from modality-specific information and constructs a consensus response domain across three modalities. Mechanism candidate augmentation identifies locally stable factors, while retrieval-geometry attribution dynamically reweights the alignment and augmentation objectives according to whether their updates preserve inter-drug discriminability.This feedback suppresses training signals that conflict with mechanism-discriminative retrieval. PMRD further combines complementary representations through reliability-aware multiview retrieval. Experiments on public datasets show improved zero-shot property prediction and more biologically coherent drug neighborhoods. Hard-negative analysis further indicates fewer conflicts between structurally dissimilar but response-related compounds. These results support PMRD as an effective framework for mechanism-aware multimodal drug representation learning.\footnote{The code will be released upon publication.}

</details>


### [58] [Dual-Domain Manifold Modeling for Hyperspectral Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25338)
*Chengxin Xie,Qiya Song,Yangbangyan Jiang,Renwei Dian,Xudong Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种双域流形建模（DDMM）框架，通过拓扑感知Transformer（TPFormer）和频域解耦的空-谱协同融合（FDSCF）模块，在空间拓扑与像素流形关系上进行联合建模，并在频域显式分离高/低频以增强几何感知高频细节，从而提升高光谱图像融合的空间结构保持与光谱重建能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高光谱图像融合方法在几何约束建模上薄弱：空间上弱的空谱交互限制了几何感知特征学习并抑制高频结构；光谱上未充分利用由光谱相似性诱导的局部流形结构，限制了像素间内在关系建模和精细光谱重建。

Method: 提出TPFormer，将全局注意力与邻域传播结合，联合建模空间拓扑与像素层流形；提出FDSCF，先用离散余弦变换将特征投影到频域并显式解耦为低/高频分量，利用低秩结构先验与谱引导的空间增强对高频进行选择性加强，从而强化空谱耦合并恢复边缘纹理。

Result: 在多种基准数据集上进行大量实验，DDMM在空间结构保持（高频细节、边缘）和光谱重建方面总体优于现有最先进方法（SoTA）。

Conclusion: DDMM通过双域流形建模、拓扑感知注意力与频域的低/高频选择性增强，有效改善了现有方法在几何约束与细节恢复方面的不足，在多个基准数据集上在空间结构保持与光谱重建上优于最新方法。

Abstract: Achieving a coherent integration of spectral richness and spatial fidelity remains a central objective in hyperspectral image fusion. However, existing hyperspectral image fusion methods struggle to effectively model geometric constraints. In the spatial domain, weak spatial-spectral interaction limits geometry-aware feature learning and suppresses high-frequency structural information, resulting in low-frequency bias and structural degradation. In the spectral domain, local manifold structures induced by spectral similarity are insufficiently exploited, limiting intrinsic pixel relationship modeling and fine-grained spectral reconstruction. To address these challenges, we propose a dual-domain manifold modeling (DDMM) framework. Specifically, we introduce a Topology-Aware Transformer (TPFormer) that combines global attention with neighborhood propagation, jointly modeling spatial topology and pixel-level feature manifold relationships to capture intrinsic spatial-spectral structures and improve topology-aware representation learning. Furthermore, a Frequency-Decoupled Spatial-Spectral Collaborative Fusion (FDSCF) module is devised, in which features are projected into the frequency domain via the discrete cosine transform and explicitly decoupled into low- and high-frequency components. Guided by a low-rank structural prior and spectral-driven spatial enhancement, FDSCF selectively enhances geometry-aware high-frequency features, strengthening spatia-spectral coupling and recovering sharper edges and finer textures. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that DDMM achieves superior overall performance over SoTA methods in terms of spatial structure preservation and spectral reconstruction.

</details>
