At these positions the disfavoured side also thinks longer. Even where the evaluation is most confident, it is not a sufficient statistic for human outcomes. The result is observational, and the causal question is left to a pre-registered randomised companion study.

</details>


### [90] [OrchBench: Evaluating Multi-Agent Orchestration Plans in Isolation via Deterministic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25656)
*Zhenzhen Ren,Jiyan He,Xinpeng Zhang,Zhenxing Qian,Ke Han,Shuxin Zheng,GuoBiao Li,Xiaoqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OrchBench是一个模拟基准，用有控制的DAG和确定性模拟器将多智能体编排计划与执行环境解耦，从而高效地评估计划质量、完工时间和代价。与真实Claude Code执行结果高度相关(r=0.816)，但耗费极少的token和时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将编排质量与工作者能力、工具可靠性和环境噪声混在一起，且真实执行随工作流规模成本高昂，需要一种能在隔离条件下、低成本地评估编排计划的办法。

Method: 从真实任务构建带依赖关系的DAG，给定每个智能体的上下文容量和智能体预算，编排器分配子任务并指定跨智能体的信息传递与保留比率。使用确定性模拟器不调用worker，输出质量、总时长和token代价等可解释指标；并将模拟得分与Claude Code的实测质量做相关性检验。

Result: 模拟分数与Claude Code执行质量Pearson相关r=0.816；模拟仅耗用真实执行的约1.3% token和10.3%墙钟时间；实验表明信息保留策略比增加智能体数量更关键，并行性收益因协调失败而减弱。

Conclusion: OrchBench能在不实际调度工作者的前提下，高效且可解释地评估多智能体编排计划，适合用于比较与诊断不同编排器；保留关键信息比增加智能体数量更能提升结果，且并行收益会因协调失败而递减。

Abstract: Complex tasks often decompose into parallelizable yet interdependent subtasks, making orchestration critical to the performance of multi-agent systems (MAS). Existing evaluations typically rely on end-to-end execution, which conflates orchestration-plan quality with worker capabilities, tool reliability, and environmental noise. Moreover, the time and token costs of real execution grow rapidly with workflow scale, making systematic evaluation expensive. We present OrchBench, a simulation-based benchmark for evaluating multi-agent orchestration plans in isolation. Starting from real-world tasks, OrchBench constructs directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) that encode task dependencies, with controlled sizes and degrees of parallelism. Given a DAG, a per-agent context limit, and an agent budget, the evaluated planner assigns subtasks to agents and specifies cross-agent information transfers and their retention ratios. A deterministic simulator evaluates the resulting plan without invoking worker agents and returns interpretable measures of result quality, makespan, and token cost. The simulated scores produced by OrchBench correlate strongly with quality scores from Claude Code executions, achieving a Pearson correlation of \(r=0.816\), while requiring only \(1.3\%\) of the tokens and \(10.3\%\) of the wall-clock time. Across diverse planners and workflow scales, we find that preserving task-critical information is more important than simply increasing the number of agents, and the benefits of parallelism diminish as coordination failures accumulate. These results establish OrchBench as an efficient and interpretable benchmark for comparing and diagnosing multi-agent orchestration plans.

</details>


### [91] [CoRT: Counterfactual Replay for Token-Level Rubric-Guided Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25659)
*Bo-Wen Zhang,Junwei He,Wen Wang,Song-Lin Lv,Wentao Ma,Rongyi Lin,Shuhan Zhong,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在带/不带 rubric 的提示下对同一响应做反事实对比，CoRT 得到 token 级的依赖性权重并把响应级优势按权重分配，从而在不增加额外打分器的情况下显著改善了 GRPO 的 token 级归因与最终性能（平均 +4.4pp）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分标准（rubric）的强化学习（GRPO）将结构化判定压缩为响应级标量回报，并将响应级优势（advantage）均匀广播到所有生成的 token，导致没有明确的机制在同一响应内分配归因，即无法区分不同标准对应的不同片段、格式或语义选择的贡献。

Method: 1) 对每个采样响应，分别在带 rubric 的提示和匹配的无 rubric 提示下计算 token 逐位 log-likelihood；2) 取两种条件下的对数似然差作为 token 依赖性代理；3) 将这些差值映射为有界且按响应归一化的权重；4) 使用这些权重把原有的响应级 GRPO 带符号优势在 token 之间重新分配以更新策略；5) 不训练额外的 token 评分模型，也不改变原始响应级回报或 GRPO 流程的其它部分。

Result: 提出 CoRT：一种用于 rubric 条件 GRPO 的 token 级别归因权重方法。CoRT 通过对同一采样响应在原始带评判标准（rubric）的提示和一个匹配的无标准提示下进行反事实重放（counterfactual replay）来重评分；基于两种上下文下的 token 逐位对数似然对比，作为对依赖 rubric 上下文程度的代理。将对比映射为有界且按响应归一化的权重，用以在不引入辅助打分器或改变响应级回报的前提下把带符号的 GRPO 优势在 token 间重新分配。实验证明，在多种指令微调模型和不同奖励粒度上，CoRT 在绝大多数对比中优于匹配的响应级 GRPO，平均提升约 4.4 个百分点，并在性能上接近需要学习的 token 级归因基线，同时避免了单独的相关性学习阶段。

Conclusion: 利用策略内部的反事实似然对比能够提供有效的响应内归因信号，从而在保持 GRPO 简单性与稳定性的同时实现更精细的 token 级信用分配；该方法在实用性与性能之间取得了良好折衷。

Abstract: Rubric-based reinforcement learning enriches language model training by evaluating model outputs against explicit criteria. Yet in GRPO-style pipelines, these structured judgments are reduced to a scalar response-level reward and converted into a response-level advantage, which is broadcast uniformly to all generated tokens. This leaves no explicit mechanism for allocating credit within a response, even when different criteria are grounded in different spans, formatting decisions, or semantic choices. We propose CoRT, a token-level credit weighting method for rubric-conditioned GRPO. Instead of training an auxiliary token scoring model, CoRT uses counterfactual replay to rescore the same sampled response under the original rubric-conditioned prompt and a matched criteria-free prompt. The resulting tokenwise log-likelihood contrasts serve as a proxy for dependence on the rubric context. CoRT maps these contrasts to bounded, response-normalized weights and uses them to redistribute the signed GRPO advantage across tokens, without introducing an auxiliary scorer or changing the response-level reward. Experiments across instruction-tuned models and reward granularities show that CoRT improves over matched response-level GRPO in the vast majority of comparisons, with an average gain of 4.4 percentage points. The method remains competitive with learned token-level credit baselines while avoiding a separate relevance-learning stage. These results suggest that policy-internal counterfactual likelihood contrasts provide an effective training signal for within-response credit allocation while retaining the simplicity and stability of GRPO.

</details>


### [92] [Localized Adaptation Reveals Distinct Learning Signatures in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25663)
*Rebecca Ramnauth,Brian Scassellati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“适应几何”概念，系统比较在不同层位（早/中/晚/全栈）添加 LoRA 适配器对五类任务（词汇绑定、事实联想、行为策略学习、因果映射、程序推理）的获取、迁移和局限性的影响，发现不同任务偏好不同适应位置，且结论在参数匹配控制和五个模型家族间大体重现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然适配通常跨深度分布，但许多目标变化是局部的；研究希望弄清适应位置如何影响学习内容、泛化能力与对原有能力的保持，以便更有针对性地设计少量参数适配策略。

Method: 构建受控基准，定义“适应几何”指标（获取、迁移、界限性），在同参数预算下对比早/中/晚层与全栈 LoRA 适配器的表现，涵盖五类目标任务，并在五个不同模型家族上复现实验。

Result: （1）词汇绑定偏好早层以实现快速获取与较好局限性，但其迁移需更广泛更新；（2）事实联想在局部适配器中更偏好后层；（3）行为学习将动作获取与策略门控在层级上分离：动作在后层获取，策略门控在中层；（4）因果与程序推理的迁移更受益于中层或全栈适配；（5）这些模式在参数匹配控制下保持，并在五个模型家族中多重复制。

Conclusion: 适配器放置的层位是控制模型学到什么、如何泛化以及保持不变的关键设计变量；不同任务有可预测的层位偏好，且存在获取—迁移—界限之间的结构性权衡。

Abstract: Transformer adaptation is typically distributed across model depth, even when the intended change is narrow. We investigate how adaptation site shapes what a model learns, how well that learning generalizes, and how selectively it is applied. We introduce a controlled benchmark spanning five objectives (lexical binding, factual association, behavioral policy learning, causal mapping, and procedural reasoning) and define each objective's "adaptation geometry" as its profile of acquisition, transfer, and boundedness under full-stack and early-, middle-, or late-layer LoRA. The objectives exhibit distinct geometries. Lexical binding favors early-layer adaptation for acquisition and boundedness but requires broader updates for transfer; factual association favors later layers among localized adapters; behavioral learning separates late-layer action acquisition from middle-layer policy gating; and causal and procedural transfer benefit most from middle- or full-stack adaptation. These patterns largely persist under parameter-matched controls, and most corresponding directional contrasts replicate across five model families. These findings establish adaptation site as a key design variable for controlling what models learn, generalize, and leave unchanged.

</details>


### [93] [OmniDelta: Skill-Driven Budget Allocation for Token Compression in OmniLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25669)
*Haoyang Huang,Wenjie Huang,Tianqi Xu,Hongyaoxing Gu,Kang Tan,Yikai Fu,Yuhao Shen,Tianyu Liu,Baolin Zhang,Jun Zhang,Xinyi Hu,Jun Dai,Shuang Ge,Lei Chen,Yue Li,Mingchen Wang,Meng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了OmniDelta——一个免训练、技能驱动的预算分配框架，通过意图感知的跨模态分配和基于内容的模态内重分配，在保持总体保留令牌比例不变的前提下，将预算分配到更有信息量的音频段和视频帧，从而在准确性—效率上取得更好折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长音视频序列带来高内存与推理成本，现有压缩方法重心在固定预算下选择重要令牌，忽视如何先分配预算（跨模态和模态内）以匹配查询意图与内容分布，且直接用查询—模态相似度或均匀分配会分别导致不可靠或冗余/遗漏关键信息。

Method: （1）构建音频与视频的技能池以刻画不同检索/理解能力对查询需求的响应；（2）意图感知的跨模态预算分配：根据查询向量与技能池的匹配将总体保留令牌预算在模态间重新分配；（3）内容感知的模态内重分配：基于局部复杂度和时间冗余在音频段与视频帧上分配局部预算；（4）将得到的局部预算与现有的令牌剪枝策略结合，保持总保留率但改变预算分布，完成推理加速。

Result: 在四个音视频基准上、使用两种Qwen2.5-Omni模型进行评测，OmniDelta在不同剪枝率下建立了新的准确率—效率Pareto前沿；以25%保留率为例，在Qwen2.5-Omni-7B上实现22.0% GPU内存节约和1.64×端到端速度提升。

Conclusion: OmniDelta在无需训练的条件下，通过构建音频/视频技能池并结合局部复杂度与时间冗余进行重分配，显著改善了OmniLLMs在高压缩率下的准确率—效率Pareto前沿；在25%保留率下对Qwen2.5-Omni-7B实现了22.0% GPU内存下降与1.64×端到端加速。

Abstract: Emerging Omni-modal Large Language Models (OmniLLMs) enable unified understanding of text, audio, and video, but their long audio-video token sequences introduce substantial memory and inference costs. Existing compression methods mainly focus on selecting important tokens under fixed budgets, leaving the preceding budget-allocation problem underexplored. We show that direct query-to-audio/video similarity is unreliable for inter-modal budget allocation, and that uniform intra-modal budgets can miss key evidence while retaining redundant content. To address these limitations, we propose OmniDelta, a training-free, skill-driven framework that couples intent-aware inter-modal allocation with content-aware intra-modal allocation. OmniDelta first constructs audio and video skill pools to shift the fixed retained-token budget according to query demand, then reallocates modality budgets over audio segments and video frames using local complexity and temporal redundancy. The resulting local budgets can be combined with existing pruning strategies, preserving the total retained-token ratio while changing where the budget is spent. Experiments on four audio-video benchmarks with two Qwen2.5-Omni models show that OmniDelta establishes a new accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontier across pruning ratios. At 25% token retention on Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, OmniDelta reduces GPU memory by 22.0% and achieves a 1.64x end-to-end speedup over full-token inference.

</details>


### [94] [DecoEvo: Score-Decoupled Co-Evolution of Solver and Rubric-Generator Skills in Text Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25675)
*Jiangwang Chen,Zixin Song,Junlin Liu,Shuaiyu Zhou,Haiyan Wu,Haihan Shi,Chenxi Zhou,Hanqing Li,Xiao Yang,Da Zhu,Guanjun Jiang,Hai Wan,Xibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DecoEvo co-evolves solver and rubric-generator under decoupled objectives using audits for coverage and discrimination, avoiding rubric-gaming and improving open-ended task performance without gold rubrics.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Text-space optimization treats LLMs as black boxes by editing natural-language artifacts (rubrics/prompts), but fixed evaluations create blind spots on open-ended tasks. Evolving rubrics by solver score risks rubric-easing (gaming) and hides omitted dimensions, so a decoupled approach is needed to expose true weaknesses.

Method: Co-evolution of two skills: a solver skill updated with criterion-level feedback, and a rubric-generator skill updated by audits measuring requirement coverage and response discrimination. Objectives are decoupled so the generator does not optimize for aggregate solver score; no gold rubrics are used. Iterative updates focus generator on uncovered requirements and on discriminating responses.

Result: Across five benchmarks and three LLM backbones, DecoEvo outperforms baselines (including SkillOpt), achieving 2.8–5.0% relative gains over SkillOpt on the five-benchmark average and best performance under official evaluations.

Conclusion: Decoupled co-evolution with complementary audits focuses optimization on newly exposed solver weaknesses and reduces wasted emphasis on already-satisfied criteria, yielding consistent performance gains across benchmarks and models.

Abstract: Text-space optimization adapts large language models (LLMs) by editing external natural-language artifacts rather than model weights, so the optimized artifacts remain inspectable and the model can be treated as a black box. However, most existing text-space methods keep evaluation fixed. On open-ended tasks, this can become a bottleneck: once the solver improves on the criteria a rubric measures, omitted dimensions remain invisible to the optimization signal. Simply evolving the rubric is also unreliable when updates are selected by the current solver's score, because apparent progress can come from making the rubric easier to satisfy. We introduce DecoEvo (Decoupled Co-Evolution), which co-evolves a solver skill and a rubric-generator skill under decoupled objectives without using gold rubrics during optimization. The solver skill is updated using criterion-level feedback, while the rubric-generator skill is revised through complementary audits of requirement coverage and response discrimination that are independent of aggregate solver score. This separation focuses generator updates on newly exposed solver weaknesses, reducing repeated emphasis on criteria the solver already satisfies. Under each benchmark's official evaluation, DecoEvo outperforms all compared methods across five benchmarks and three LLM backbones, yielding 2.8--5.0\% relative gains over SkillOpt in the five-benchmark average.

</details>


### [95] [Cognivia: A Cognitive Behavioral Therapy Copilot for Evidence-Based Mental Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25681)
*Qi Chen,Siria Xiyueyao Luo,Jian Wang,Yuan Shi,Haocong Rao,Xuejiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Cognivia结合权威CBT资源与专家监督，微调轻量级LLM以实现可解释的认知扭曲识别与理性回应，且引入层级化评估框架，在自动与人类评估上表现优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缓解认知扭曲以改善心理健康；CBT有效但受专业治疗师短缺限制；现有LLM精神健康应用存在领域特异性不足、回应过度恭维、缺乏认知扭曲标注等问题。

Method: 汇集权威CBT文本与心理健康Q&A数据，设计多阶段提示和结构化生成策略在行为科学专家监督下构建增强数据集，微调轻量LLM得到Cognivia；提出并应用层级化质量评估框架，使用词汇指标、LLM评审（双准则）及10位专家的人工评估比较基线。

Result: 提出Cognivia：基于权威CBT文本构建增强数据集，加入心理健康Q&A，采用多阶段提示与结构化生成并由行为科学专家监督，微调轻量LLM以实现自动认知扭曲识别与理性回应生成；同时提出首个层级化质量评估框架并通过词汇指标、LLM评审与10位行为科学专家的人类评估验证，优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 方法在识别认知扭曲与生成理性回应方面有效，受限于专家样本规模与潜在泛化与安全性问题，但为领域特定AI治疗师与评估标准化提供了可复现路径。

Abstract: Cognitive distortion amplifies negative emotions and contributes to mental health disorders. Cognitive Behavioral Therapy (CBT) is an effective way to address cognitive distortions, but its large-scale application is limited by the shortage of professional therapists. Although large language models (LLMs) have recently been explored for mental health applications, existing methods still suffer from limited domain specificity, overly flattering responses, and the absence of well-defined annotations for cognitive distortions. This paper proposes Cognivia, an evidence-based artificial intelligence therapist that integrates automatic cognitive distortion identification and rational response generation. Our framework is built on authoritative CBT texts widely regarded as core paradigms and standard references. It is further augmented with mental health question-answer (Q and A) data, and employs multi-stage prompting and structured generation strategies under the supervision of behavioral science experts. Then we fine-tune a lightweight LLM on this augmented CBT dataset to obtain Cognivia. In addition, we propose the first hierarchical quality evaluation framework for assessing LLM-generated rational responses, developed through collaboration between AI researchers and behavioral science experts. Cognivia is evaluated using lexical metrics, LLM-based Judges with two complementary criteria, and human evaluation by 10 behavioral science experts. It consistently outperforms the baseline methods in cognitive distortion recognition and rational response generation, demonstrating its effectiveness. Our code is available at https://github.com/SNOWTEAM2023/Cognivia.

</details>


### [96] [Nudging Sustainable Choices through LLM-Generated Recommendation Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25726)
*Haya Halimeh,Dietmar Jannach,Oliver Müller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用生成式AI生成的以行为干预为框架的可持续性解释比单纯披露更能促进行为改变；而单纯披露提升的是感知评价而非选择行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将推荐系统中的解释功能由纯信息呈现扩展为行为助推工具，探究通过不同框架呈现可持续信息能否在决策时直接促进行为改变，同时检验生成式AI在自动生成理论驱动解释方面的可行性。

Method: 使用生成式AI生成基于助推理论的可持续性解释，经过人工与LLM审查后，在两种涉入度不同的领域（速溶咖啡与酒店预订）开展随机对照实验（N=529），比较不同解释类型对选择与感知的影响。

Result: Authors show LLM-generated, nudge-informed recommendation explanations can increase sustainable choices when framed or invoking descriptive social norms, but plain disclosure alone does not; perception and behavior can diverge.

Conclusion: 解释的行为化表述（尤其是描述性规范）在推荐场景中能显著提高可持续选项的选择率并简化决策，但仅披露信息不足以改变行为。LLMs可用于规模化生成基于理论的解释，为自适应解释设计提供途径。

Abstract: Recommender systems mediate everyday consumption, offering a promising channel for encouraging sustainable choices. Prior research shows that explanations influence users' perceptions of recommendations and can support more informed decisions. We argue that explanations can also serve as behavioral nudges by foregrounding sustainability information at the moment of choice. This study investigates how different behavioral framings of sustainability information in recommendation explanations affect user choices and perceptions. Using generative AI, we generate sustainability-aware explanations by drawing on nudge theory and validate them through human evaluation and LLM-as-a-judge audits. Building on this foundation, we conduct two randomized studies ($N = 529$) in a low involvement domain (instant coffee) and a high involvement domain (hotel bookings), in which participants choose among preference matched recommendations accompanied by these explanations. Our results show that, across both domains, merely disclosing sustainability information in explanations does not change choices, whereas framing that information or invoking a descriptive social norm significantly increases sustainable selections and eases decision-making. Notably, perception and behavior diverge, as plain disclosure improves explanation evaluations without translating into more sustainable selection behavior. Our work demonstrates how LLMs can generate theory-grounded explanations at scale, pointing toward practical explanation-based interventions for social good. We conclude by discussing implications for adaptive explanation design with generative AI.

</details>


### [97] [Loss Invariance Determines What Concept Layers Encode: Volume Grounding in Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25748)
*Hyunkyung Han,Min Jung Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 仅用目标输出监督时，概念预测看似相关但可失去物理量纲（被按比例塌缩），因此单纯的概念准确度不足以保证可解释变量的物理有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验概念瓶颈模型（concept bottleneck models）中仅以概念预测准确度评估是否足够——以心脏超声视频中用左室舒张末与收缩末容积（EDV/ESV）作为“概念”来推断射血分数（EF）为例。

Method: 构建视频Transformer编码器，输出EDV与ESV作为概念层，按解析式计算EF且无残差直连输出；比较仅以EF为目标与同时监督体积（mL）的训练方案，在1276例外部测试研究上评估MAE、体积分布和相关性。

Result: 在公开超声数据集上训练视频Transformer并以EDV/ESV作为中间概念，直接用EF目标与同时监督体积（毫升）两种训练对比。结果显示：概念瓶颈并未提高EF预测误差（MAE 6.89 vs 7.13）；但在仅以EF监督时，预测的体积分布几乎塌缩至0.1毫升（参考分布为35.7和45.7毫升），尽管相关性部分保留。该现象源于EF=1−ESV/EDV的比例不变性：若EDV与ESV同时按同一比例缩放，EF不变，故仅以EF为目标时概念层仅由目标确定到比例因子。加入绝对单位监督后，体积绝对误差从89.8降至25.8毫升，但EF误差增加约0.4。

Conclusion: 评估临床可解释中间变量时，必须检验训练目标的不变性结构与可识别性，必要时用绝对单位监督或引入约束以恢复量纲和可解释性。

Abstract: Objective: Concept bottleneck models route prediction through interpretable intermediate variables, and their validity is normally judged by how accurately those variables are predicted. We ask whether that judgement is sufficient, using left ventricular volumes as the concepts underlying ejection fraction estimation from echocardiographic video.
  Methods: A video transformer encoder was trained on a publicly available echocardiography dataset. End-systolic and end-diastolic volumes formed a concept layer from which ejection fraction was computed analytically, with no residual path to the output. We compared training under an ejection fraction objective alone against training with additional supervision of the volumes in millilitres, and evaluated both on 1276 held-out studies.
  Results: The concept bottleneck did not increase ejection fraction error relative to direct regression, at 6.89 against 7.13 mean absolute error. Without volume supervision, however, the spread of predicted volumes collapsed to 0.1 millilitres against reference spreads of 35.7 and 45.7 millilitres, while correlation was partly preserved. We show that this follows from an invariance property of the objective: ejection fraction is a ratio and is unchanged when both volumes are rescaled, so the loss determines the concept layer only up to scale. Supervision in absolute units reduced volume error from 89.8 to 25.8 millilitres at a cost of 0.4 in ejection fraction error.
  Conclusion: Concept accuracy alone can conceal a concept layer that carries no physical scale.
  Significance: Interpretable intermediate variables in clinical models should be validated against the invariance structure of the training objective, not only against prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [98] [Speculate While You Reason: Teaching Agents to Predict Their Next Tool Call via Joint Agent-Speculator RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25816)
*Jiabao Ji,Yujian Liu,Li An,Rohit Jain,Gungor Polatkan,Siyu Zhu,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将代理与其工具调用预测器统一为同一模型（self-speculating agent），通过联合强化学习在agent与speculator两种模式间交替训练，并重用prefix KV cache，从而提高下一个工具调用的预测准确率并隐藏工具调用延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具调用猜测器通常与部署代理不一致（draft模型或缓存轨迹），造成speculator-agent gap并限制猜测效果；观察到目标代理自身已经是强猜测器，因而提出统一设计以消除差距并更高效地隐藏工具调用延迟。

Method: 设计单模型双模式体系：agent模式执行任务、speculator模式从部分轨迹预测下一工具调用；完全重用prefix KV cache以节省开销；采用joint agent-speculator强化学习，交替进行agent与speculator更新，猜测目标来自代理自身的rollouts。

Result: 在agentic搜索问答和会话工具使用任务上，方法将Qwen3-4B的下一个工具调用Hit@1从44.1提升到61.2，Qwen3.5-4B从48.9提升到66.3，同时保留了代理任务成功率。

Conclusion: 将speculator与代理统一为单一模型，通过从代理rollout派生猜测目标并联合训练，可显著提升下一步工具调用的Hit@1，同时保持代理任务成功率不下降。

Abstract: Large language model agents often spend substantial wall-clock time waiting for tool call results. Tool-call speculation can hide this latency by predicting and pre-executing an agent's next tool call if the prediction matches the agent's eventual tool call, but existing speculators are typically separate draft models or cached traces that are poorly aligned with the deployed agent's own behavior. We identify this speculator-agent gap and show that the target agent itself is a strong next-call speculator. This points to a simpler design: unifying the agent and speculator within the same model. In this paper, we introduce the self-speculating agent, a single model that both solves tasks in agent mode and predicts its next tool call from partial trajectories in speculator mode, fully reusing prefix KV cache. To enable this dual-mode agent without degrading performance, we propose a joint agent-speculator reinforcement learning method, which derives speculation targets from the agent's own rollouts and alternates agent and speculator updates. Across agentic search QA and conversational tool-use agentic tasks, our method improves average next tool-call Hit@1 from 44.1 to 61.2 for Qwen3-4B and from 48.9 to 66.3 for Qwen3.5-4B, while preserving agent task success.

</details>


### [99] [Distributed Constraint Optimization via Online Learning and Iterative Pricing with Application to Large-Scale Satellite Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25835)
*Itai Zilberstein,Pranav Rajbhandari,Steve Chien,Tuomas Sandholm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将现代在线学习/博弈平衡算法移植到DCOP，并提出元层任务分配+局部调度的分解框架与迭代定价耦合方法，在真实分布式卫星调度上显著优于现有方法（>99% vs 87%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 很多实际DCOP规模巨大难以整体求解，尤其在通信受限的分布式系统（如卫星编队）中。需要可扩展且通信高效的方法，既能保证分配决策质量，又能有效解决每个实体的局部调度问题。

Method: （1）复现并改造潜在博弈与现代在线学习/均衡寻求算法以解不完整DCOP；（2）引入两层分解：高层元DCOP负责任务分配，低层独立求解局部调度问题；（3）设计迭代定价机制：低层将局部最优反馈为价格/效用，元层据此更新并重新分配任务，迭代直到收敛；（4）将在线学习算法作为元层决策或局部协调器以提高收敛与解质量；并在真实卫星调度数据集上进行比较实验。

Result: 提出结合在线学习与分解框架用于求解大规模分布式约束优化问题（DCOP），并应用于分布式卫星调度；提出两层分解：元DCOP（任务分配）与独立局部优化（调度），以及一种基于迭代定价（iterative pricing）的耦合机制；将现代在线博弈/平衡点求解算法适配于DCOP并证明其与代表性不完整算法具有可比性能；在实际卫星调度实例上，结合在线学习与迭代定价达成了超过99%观测请求满足率，优于现有基线的87%。

Conclusion: 本文展示了两条互补路径：一是将在线学习用于DCOP求解，二是提出基于迭代定价的分解框架以扩展到大规模问题。结合两者在真实卫星调度上接近最优的表现，证明了方法实用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Distributed constraint optimization problems (DCOPs) provide a popular framework for distributed decision making under limited communication, but many real-world instances are too large to solve monolithically. We address this challenge from two complementary directions. We revisit the connection between DCOPs and potential games, and adapt modern online learning algorithms for equilibrium finding to DCOPs. We show that these algorithms are competitive with representative incomplete DCOP algorithms. We then turn to decomposition frameworks for large-scale DCOPs, motivated by large-scale decentralized satellite scheduling. We propose a new framework that separates a DCOP into two interacting subproblems: a high-level meta-DCOP for task allocation, and independent local optimization problems for scheduling. To couple the two levels, we develop a novel iterative pricing method that updates the meta-level utilities using feedback from the local optimizers. Combining our online learning methods with our iterative pricing framework, we obtain near-optimal performance on real-world decentralized satellite scheduling problem instances, fulfilling over 99% of observation requests compared with 87% for state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [100] [HiSkill: Empowering LLM Agents with Hierarchical Skill Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25853)
*Yu Hao,Jinxuan Cai,Qi Zhang,Yawen Li,Zhiqiang Zhang,Chuan Shi,Cheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiSkill 构建了一个层次化技能图（技能节点、AtomicOp 节点和带类型的边），把高层可复用技能与可执行动作模板连接起来，捕捉分解、时间转移、兼容性、支持和恢复等关系。推理时检索任务相关子图，利用符号任务状态与子图引导 LLM 在技能切换、AtomicOp 选择和动作落地间迭代执行。实验证明在三种交互环境上优于 SOTA 并降低推理令牌消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹到技能的方法通常返回独立的高层文本技能集合，忽视技能间的关系且与可执行动作之间存在脱节，限制了在长时序交互任务中复用经验的能力。

Method: 构建有向图：技能节点连接到 AtomicOp（可执行模板），边带类型表示分解、时间顺序、兼容性、支持和恢复关系。推理阶段检索紧凑的任务相关子图，用符号任务状态和当前激活技能指导 LLM 迭代切换技能、选择 AtomicOp 并将其落地为具体动作。

Result: 在三种交互环境上，HiSkill 比现有基线表现更好并减少推理时的令牌消耗；作者已公开数据与代码。

Conclusion: HiSkill 通过层次化技能图有效弥合了高层技能与可执行动作之间的鸿沟，从而在长时序交互任务中提升性能并提高推理效率。

Abstract: Skills have become an important abstraction for enabling large language model (LLM) agents to reuse past experience in long-horizon interactive tasks. However, existing trajectory-to-skill methods often produce flat collections of high-level textual skills that are stored and retrieved independently, leaving skill relations underutilized and maintaining a gap between high-level skills and executable actions. In this paper, we propose HiSkill, a hierarchical skill graph framework that organizes interaction trajectories into a directed graph with skill nodes, AtomicOp nodes, and typed edges. Specifically, the graph connects reusable high-level skills with executable action templates, while also capturing decomposition, temporal transition, compatibility, support, and recovery relations among them. At inference time, HiSkill retrieves a compact task-relevant subgraph and performs subgraph-guided task execution, where a symbolic task state, an active skill, and the retrieved subgraph guide the LLM agent to switch skills, select AtomicOps, and ground executable actions iteratively. Experiments on three interactive environments show that HiSkill outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while reducing inference token consumption, demonstrating the effectiveness of bridging high-level skills and executable action grounding through a hierarchical skill graph. Our data and code is available at https://github.com/BUPT-GAMMA/HiSkill.

</details>


### [101] [Runtime Uncertainty Monitoring for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems Using Bayesian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25877)
*Bart Custers,Koorosh Aslansefat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用多智能体+token级log-prob校准+贝叶斯网络的组合来对LLM驱动的精算工作流进行不确定性传播，保持基线性能并增加运行时置信度与稳定性可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精算建模为高风险决策支持场景，LLM的概率性输出及智能体间依赖会引入不确定性，错误输出可能导致错误风险评估、不公平定价和法规不合规，因此需要可解释、可传播的不确定性量化机制。

Method: 构建以中心枢纽协调的多智能体系统：各专责智能体负责数据准备、建模、审查和解释。使用基于token的对数概率（length-normalised）汇总，经校准后作为任务级置信度输入到贝叶斯网络以实现不确定性传播。强调不直接把log-prob当作正确概率，而是先转换为校准置信度。

Result: 该框架能重现基线精算性能（即不显著降低模型效能），同时提供工作流稳定性度量和运行时不确定性传播的可视化/量化信息；并展示了校准log-prob步骤在结果可靠性中的关键作用。

Conclusion: 提出的多智能体框架在保持精算建模基线性能的同时，能对工作流中的不确定性进行传播与量化，从而为决策支持提供额外的稳定性和运行时置信度信息。

Abstract: This paper investigates how multi-agent systems (MAS)-based on large language models (LLMs) can support actuarial risk modelling, with a particular focus on uncertainty quantification. Actuarial workflows represent a high-stakes decision-support setting where unreliable outputs may lead to incorrect risk assessment, unfair pricing, and regulatory non-compliance. To address uncertainty introduced by the probabilistic nature of LLMs and dependencies between agents, a multi-agent framework is proposed in which specialised agents perform data preparation, modelling, review, and explanation tasks under a central hub. The main contribution is a novel approach to uncertainty propagation using token-level log-probabilities and a Bayesian Network. Importantly, log probabilities are not treated as direct probabilities of correctness or task success. Instead, length-normalised log-probability summaries are transformed into calibrated task-level confidence estimates before incorporation into the Bayesian Network. Results show that the framework reproduces baseline actuarial performance while providing additional insight into workflow stability and runtime uncertainty propagation.

</details>


### [102] [Distributing Security Controls Through Harness Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25890)
*William Robert Gore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A distributable agent harness (SHarD) can carry off‑the‑shelf security controls to commercial AI coding agents at scale, matching vendor‑native secure configurations on a 23‑test OWASP‑based suite; nondeterminism and cross‑boundary autonomy are notable issues.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Security and risk concerns block organizational adoption of agentic AI; vendor-native controls create ecosystem lock‑in; the paper asks whether off‑the‑shelf controls can be embedded and distributed to a distributed user base via a custom harness (SHarD) without losing efficacy.

Method: Phased black-box testing across four agent configurations (two commercial agents with/without vendor controls, a baseline harness, and a security‑hardened harness), using a 23‑case test suite mapped to the OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications; measured efficacy via an "adjusted score" metric and compared behaviors across configurations.

Result: SHarD (a distributable harness built on Pi) successfully embedded three control classes—OS sandboxing, skill scanning, and tool restriction—and delivered equivalent efficacy to direct secure commercial agent installations. SHarD achieved an adjusted score of 100%, matched the best securely configured commercial agent, produced no regression across test categories, and revealed that model nondeterminism and autonomous boundary crossings affect security outcomes.

Conclusion: Embedding OS sandboxing, skill scanning, and tool restriction in a single installable harness is feasible and effective; authors propose initial criteria for a control‑harness fitness framework and identify a new research question for follow‑up (likely about robustness, nondeterminism, or distributional issues).

Abstract: AI coding agents are being adopted at historic speed, yet security and risk concerns remain the primary barrier to scaling agentic AI across organizations. Existing security controls for coding agents are not systematically distributed to engineering teams, and vendor-native solutions introduce ecosystem dependencies that may not suit every deployment context. This paper investigates whether off-the-shelf security controls can be implemented on commercial AI coding agents and scaled to a distributed user base via a custom agent harness. A phased testing methodology was applied across four agent configurations --- two commercial agents with and without controls, a baseline harness, and a security-hardened harness --- using a 23-test suite derived from the OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications. SHarD (Secure Harness Distribution), a distributable harness built on the Pi agent harness, demonstrated that three categories of security controls --- OS sandboxing, skill scanning, and tool restriction --- can be embedded and distributed via a single install command while retaining equivalent efficacy to direct installation on commercial agents. SHarD achieved an adjusted score of 100\%, matching the best securely configured commercial agent, with no regression across any test category. Notable observations include evidence that model non-determinism produces inconsistent security outcomes and that autonomous agent behavior can cross system boundaries in ways that OS sandboxing directly mitigates. Initial characteristics toward a control harness fitness framework are proposed, and a third research question is identified for future investigation.

</details>


### [103] [Messier: A High-Resolution Corpus for Cross-Benchmark Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25891)
*Stefan Krsteski,Charlotte Meyer,Guillaume Allegre,Tony O'Halloran,Alexandre Sallinen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Messier 是一个统一化、接近百万条记录的评估语料库，通过标准化记录与反事实重评分揭示基准评估中的可比性与聚合偏差问题，并生成与现有能力指数高度相关的能力量表，推动代理评估的可复用与审计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前交互式环境中 AI 代理评估高度碎片化（任务、支架、验证器与评分规则各异），导致实证记录不可比、规模有限或需昂贵重跑。需要一个统一、可复用且可扩展的评估语料库以支持能力刻度、审计与细粒度失败分析。

Method: 收集并标准化了 957,253 条公开和补充的评估记录，覆盖 30 个基准、714 个代理、11,891 个任务与 74,205 个验证器；对每条记录按模型、支架（scaffold）、环境、任务、验证器与聚合规则进行结构化；补充了 5-agent 运行覆盖 6 个少被代表的专业与科学领域（含最新法律基准）；为职业/行业分析添加 SOC/NAICS 分类；并使用反事实重评分研究不同聚合规则对性能理解的影响，同时从记录中推导能力量表并与 Epoch 指数比较。

Result: 产出 Messier 语料库（957k 条记录）并公开化其结构化格式；发现不同基准类型的前沿进展不均衡；证明严格的全通过聚合规则会掩盖进步并影响排名；构建与 Epoch 指数高度一致的能力量表并可按领域/职业/动作空间/验证器类型细化；提供可用于基准审计、能力扩展与失败诊断的基础设施。

Conclusion: Messier 建立了一个统一且大规模的评估记录语料库，使得不同任务、验证器和聚合规则下的代理表现可比，并揭示了前沿能力在基准类型间的不均衡进展（例如函数调用趋于饱和、编程进步最快、企业工作流最具挑战性）。同时，通过反事实重评分（counterfactual rescoring）指出了“全部通过”式多验证器聚合可能掩盖真实进步并扭曲代理排名。最终，从标准化记录推导出的能力量表与现有指数高度相关（Spearman ρ=0.81），为能力刻画、基准审计与评测失败的细粒度分析提供了基础设施。

Abstract: Evaluating AI agents in interactive environments is hindered by fragmented tasks, scaffolds, verifiers, and scoring rules. Existing efforts focus on narrow settings, remain limited in scale, or require costly reruns, leaving much of the empirical record incomparable. We introduce Messier, a unified corpus of 957,253 records that span 30 benchmarks, 714 agents, 11,891 tasks, and 74,205 verifiers. Messier consolidates public benchmark scores and supplements them with five-agent runs across six underrepresented professional and scientific domains, including a recent legal benchmark. Each record is standardized by model, scaffold, environment, task, verifier, and aggregation rule, with SOC/NAICS classifications for occupational and industry analysis. Using this corpus, we show frontier progress is uneven across benchmark types, with "function calling" saturated, "programming" improving the fastest, and "enterprise workflows" remaining the most challenging. Furthermore, counterfactual rescoring shows that strict all-pass aggregation in multi-verifier tasks can obscure progress and artificially alter agent rankings. From these standardized records, we derive capability scales that align with Epoch's Evaluation Capability Index rankings at Spearman \r{ho} = 0.81 and can be specialized by domain, occupation, action space, or verifier type. Messier provides a foundational, reusable infrastructure for agent capability scaling, benchmark auditing, and fine-grained analysis of evaluation failures.

</details>


### [104] [Interactive Reward Agent: GUI Task Evaluation via Environment-State Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25904)
*Chenrui Shi,Yuwei Wu,Yang Liu,Ruining Feng,Zirui Shang,Zhi Gao,Lifeng Fan,Che Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种交互式奖励评估器（Interactive Reward Agent, IRA），采用“先提出（propose）再验证（verify）”的流程：在任务指令和执行后环境下，先生成任务完成条件，再通过系统/应用/GUI工具调用验证证据，从界面可见信息与环境状态中综合判断。构建了GUI-RewardBench（321条轨迹，涵盖10类Ubuntu桌面应用）。在该基准上IRA达86.9%准确率，且用于强化学习时在OSWorld上达34.0%成功率，优于现有评估器基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠执行轨迹截图往往无法获得完成任务所需的环境信息（如系统配置、文件内容、应用设置），因此需要一种能访问并验证环境状态的自动化评估方法，以提供更可靠的奖励信号用于测试和训练GUI代理。

Method: 基于propose-then-verify框架：给定任务指令和执行后环境（包括截图与系统状态），第一步生成候选完成条件（任务目标或检查点），第二步通过调用系统命令、应用内接口和GUI自动化工具去检索或操作环境以获取证据，合并可见界面与环境状态的证据后输出评估结果。并构建并评测了GUI-RewardBench基准用于量化性能。

Result: 在GUI-RewardBench上IRA达到86.9%准确率，优于现有评估器；将IRA作为奖励用于强化学习可使GUI代理在OSWorld上的成功率达到34.0%，表明其能提供有效的训练信号。

Conclusion: 通过将界面观测与环境工具调用结合的交互式验证流程，IRA能更可靠地判断GUI任务是否完成，显著优于仅依赖截图的评估器，并能作为有效的奖励信号用于训练GUI代理。

Abstract: Graphical user interface task evaluation aims to determine whether a GUI agent has successfully completed a user instruction. Automated GUI task evaluation has received increasing attention because the evaluation results can serve as reward signals for both test-time scaling and post-training. However, reliable GUI task evaluation remains challenging because the judgments often require access to environment states, such as system configurations, file data, and application settings, beyond the screenshots of execution trajectories. In this paper, we propose an interactive reward agent (IRA) based on a propose-then-verify framework to acquire and verify evidence from the post-execution environment. Given a task instruction and a GUI environment after the GUI agent execution, IRA first proposes the task completion conditions and then verifies them by invoking system tools, application tools, and GUI tools. This design combines evidence from both visible interfaces and the environment state in an interactive process. We further introduce GUI-RewardBench, a benchmark of 321 GUI task trajectories spanning 10 Ubuntu desktop application categories. Experiments show that IRA achieves 86.9% accuracy on GUI-RewardBench, outperforming existing evaluator baselines. We further apply IRA to reinforcement learning of GUI agents, achieving a 34.0% OSWorld success rate, which demonstrates that IRA can provide effective reward signals for training GUI agents.

</details>


### [105] [Toward Standardized Cross-Vendor Agent Tool Trust Management in Autonomous Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25914)
*Ravi Kant Sharma,Ashutosh Uttam,Ajay Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为多厂商自治网络提出标准化跨厂商工具信任管理模型（AgentToolMO），在现有3GPP管理框架内实现可证明的级联控制与跨域通知，仿真验证具有实时约束与良好扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多供应商自治网络中AI代理跨厂商调用工具时缺乏统一的可信可见性机制，避免单一供应商工具被攻陷后引发级联服务影响的问题。

Method: 定义形式化信任状态机并给出收敛性/阻尼性分析，设计基于MnS的跨域通知与依赖图遍历算法，采用多厂商网络拓扑仿真评估通知延迟、级联传播、收敛迭代数与通知规模等指标。

Result: 提出AgentToolMO，一个面向3GPP NRM的代理工具信任管理信息模型；包含形式化信任状态机（带可证的渐进强制）、阻尼级联传播与有界收敛、通过现有管理服务（MnS）接口的跨厂商信任通知，以及通过NRM依赖图的事后影响评估；仿真实验在多厂商拓扑上显示可将无感知蔓延从小时级降至接近实时，收敛有界、通知规模亚线性。

Conclusion: AgentToolMO在理论和仿真层面展示了通过标准化跨厂商通知与受控级联机制显著提高自治网络的抗冲击力，提供了可行的3GPP标准化路径，但需补强实际部署、安全认证、厂商激励和更广泛的实测验证。

Abstract: Autonomous Network Levels 4-5 require AI agents to invoke tools across vendor boundaries without human oversight, yet existing management standards lack a standardized mechanism for cross-vendor trust visibility. When a tool from Vendor B is compromised, agents from Vendor A continue invoking it -- unaware of the trust degradation -- causing cascading service impact. We present AgentToolMO, a proposed 3GPP NRM information model for agent tool trust management. The model comprises: a formally defined trust state machine with provable graduated enforcement, damped cascade propagation with bounded convergence, cross-vendor trust notifications via existing Management Services (MnS) interfaces, and retroactive impact assessment through NRM dependency graph traversal. Simulation-based evaluation across multi-vendor topologies shows that standardized cross-vendor notifications reduce blast radius from hours-scale undetected propagation to near-real-time containment bounded by MnS notification delivery, with cascade convergence guaranteed in bounded iterations and sub-linear notification scaling across vendor domains. The framework operates within existing 3GPP management infrastructure, leverages existing protocols, and provides a standardization pathway for trustworthy multi-vendor autonomous network management.

</details>


### [106] [Penelope: Localized Latent Recurrence for Efficient Structured Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25915)
*Yutong Chen,Shouqian Shi,Xinran Liu,Haochen Wang,Jiaying Wang,Tianxing Xu,Yuanxi Wang,Zirui Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在解码器有限区间内用内部循环（time-modulated GRU）进行潜在推理，减少重复完整解码器运行及长可见CoT，达成准确率与效能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Provide extra reasoning computation for structured tasks without increasing model size or tying computation cost to autoregressive output length (CoT), thereby reducing training/deployment costs and inference latency.

Method: Evaluate lower decoder prefix once to form a boundary memory; iteratively refine that memory using time-modulated GRU dynamics and recurrent readout states; employ a progressive CoT-to-latent curriculum to transfer visible reasoning into latent path; finally generate answers without serializing long CoT tokens.

Result: Penelope localizes recurrent latent reasoning to a selected decoder interval, building a boundary memory from a single lower-prefix decoder evaluation and refining it with time-modulated GRU dynamics and recurrent readouts, enabling additional computation in latent space without long visible CoT traces; achieves competitive accuracy and lower latency on structured reasoning benchmarks.

Conclusion: Latent refinement can be confined to a narrow decoder interval to reduce repeated full-decoder execution and long visible reasoning traces, providing a practical accuracy-efficiency tradeoff for decoder-only Transformers.

Abstract: Complex structured reasoning tasks often require additional computation, yet current language models obtain it mainly by increasing parameter scale or by serializing intermediate steps as chain-of-thought (CoT) tokens. The former raises training and deployment costs, while the latter ties reasoning computation to autoregressive output length. We introduce Penelope, an efficient latent-reasoning framework for pretrained decoder-only Transformers that localizes recurrent computation to a selected decoder interval. The lower decoder prefix is evaluated once to construct a problem-conditioned boundary memory, which is then iteratively refined through time-modulated GRU dynamics and recurrent readout states before answer generation. A progressive CoT-to-latent curriculum transfers visible reasoning into this internal recurrent path, allowing additional computation to be allocated in latent space without repeatedly executing the complete decoder or generating a long intermediate trace. Experiments on open-source structured-reasoning benchmarks show that, at validation-selected latent budgets, Penelope attains competitive accuracy relative to established latent-reasoning models while reducing measured inference latency. These results show that latent refinement can be localized to a narrow decoder interval, reducing repeated full-decoder execution without generating a long visible reasoning trace and providing a practical accuracy-efficiency tradeoff for decoder-only Transformer models.

</details>


### [107] [dtControl2+$\varepsilon$: Trading Optimality for Explainability in MDPs via Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25925)
*Tereza Kinská,Jan Křetínský,Tobias Meggendorfer,Sabine Rieder,Maximilian Weininger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过允许有限的性能损失 ε，将控制策略蒸馏为大幅更小的决策树，同时保留 ε-最优性保证，从而得到可调的、更易理解的控制器表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使是现有的“可解释”决策树策略，在大型系统或角落案例多时仍会产生过大的树结构，难以为人理解；而简单的压缩可能破坏关键决策并导致错误控制。引入可控的性能松弛 ε，旨在在保证近似最优性的前提下，提取出更精炼、可理解的策略要点。

Method: 在马尔可夫决策过程(MDP)设定下，将现有的可解释控制器生成工具 dtControl2 扩展为支持可调容差 ε 的策略精简方法。该方法通过在决策树学习/蒸馏过程中引入 ε-最优性约束（即允许的性能下降上界），在剪枝或合并状态-动作分区时用性能保证替代精确一致性，从而生成更小的决策树。实现上可能结合了策略价值估计/模型检验来判断合并是否满足 ε-最优性，并在训练或后处理阶段进行结构压缩。

Result: 提出并实现了带 ε-最优性保证的决策树生成/蒸馏方法（扩展 dtControl2），实验表明在多种任务上生成的决策树比现有方法小几个数量级，同时仍满足给定的 ε-最优性约束，从而实现了可调节的可解释性/精确性权衡。

Conclusion: 引入 ε-最优性作为压缩准则，使得决策树策略在可解释性与性能之间有明确的权衡，能显著降低复杂度并保持近似最优性能；该方案适合需要人类可理解解释的控制场景，但需权衡 ε 的选择以及验证成本。

Abstract: Over the past decade, decision trees have been used to represent controllers (a.k.a. policies) in an explainable way, with dtControl2 as a current state-of-the-art tool. However, for systems that are large or have many corner cases, even such representations tend to be too complex and not human-comprehensible. Unfortunately, reducing the size of the decision tree is not straightforward, as missing just a single crucial case might result in an incorrect controller. We tackle this issue in the setting of Markov decision processes, extending dtControl2 by "$\varepsilon$" functionality: Given an allowed imprecision $\varepsilon \geq 0$, we construct a smaller decision tree, distilling the essence of the controller, while still guaranteeing its $\varepsilon$-optimality. This enables us to provide tunably simpler explanations, omitting a controllable amount of detail. Our tool constructs decision trees that are orders of magnitude smaller than the state of the art.

</details>


### [108] [A Cost-Effective Multimodal LLM Reasoning Framework for Question Answering over Irregular Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25947)
*Frank Nie,Ethan B Liu,Yuan Zhu,Wei Fan,Jindong Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ClinPRISM，一种针对不规则临床时间序列（ICTS）问答的高效多模态LLM推理框架，通过多尺度不规则感知编码器、时间证据蒸馏器与渐进对齐策略，将稀疏异步观测压缩为少量LLM兼容token，在4B模型上以16个时间序列token实现SOTA与0.15s的平均推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态时间序列LLM难以处理临床数据的稀疏性、异步性和不规则采样，影响临床问答的准确性与效率，因而需要一种既能保留关键时序证据又能高效满足LLM输入约束的解决方案。

Method: 1) 不规则感知多尺度编码器：对稀疏临床证据在多个时间尺度建模以捕获长短期特征；2) 时间证据蒸馏器：跨尺度整合并压缩表征，输出少量LLM兼容token；3) 渐进对齐策略：按序将不规则轨迹对齐到LLM的文本嵌入空间；4) 构建合成/注释数据集（30k时间序列、41k指令微调样本）并在4B参数骨干模型上训练与评估。

Result: 在保留仅16个时间序列token的情况下，于持出评测基准上达到SOTA，平均每问推理延迟0.15秒（基于4B模型），显示出显著的效率-性能折衷。

Conclusion: ClinPRISM在保留关键临床时序信息的前提下，能高效地将不规则时序压缩为少量可被大模型处理的token，因而在受控基准上达到或超过现有方法的性能，同时显著降低计算与延迟开销。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) over irregular clinical time series (ICTS) plays a pivotal role in a wide range of healthcare applications. Although recent multimodal time-series large language models (LLMs) have shown considerable promise in general-purpose time-series QA, they remain poorly equipped to model the sparsity, asynchrony, and irregular sampling patterns of clinical observations. To fill this gap, we propose ClinPRISM, a cost-effective multimodal LLM reasoning framework for question answering over ICTS data. First, we devise an irregularity-aware multi-scale encoder to capture sparse clinical evidence at diverse temporal scales. Then, we propose a temporal evidence distiller to integrate representations across these scales and compress them into a small number of LLM-compatible tokens. Moreover, we introduce a progressive alignment strategy that sequentially aligns the irregular trajectories with the LLM's textual embedding space. To facilitate training, we construct 30,000 clinical time series paired with multi-scale descriptions, together with 41,000 instruction-tuning instances spanning 11 tasks. Using a 4-billion-parameter LLM backbone, ClinPRISM achieves state-of-the-art performance on the held-out evaluation benchmark while using only 16 time-series tokens and achieving an average inference latency of 0.15 seconds per question.

</details>


### [109] [Large Language Model for Operations Research Formulation Selection in Multi-Warehouse Inventory Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25956)
*Jintao Xu,Yingzheng Ma,Jiong Dong,Yongzhi Qi,Jianshen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文把多仓库存分配问题视为实例级的OR表述选择任务，提出以求解器反馈为奖励的LLM选择器训练流程（SFT→IPO偏好构造→基于元数据的GRPO），在工业数据上显著优于固定表述和基线选择器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一MIP表述无法稳健覆盖由需求集中度、库存不平衡、补货规模、服务约束和预测波动等诱发的多样实例级场景，因而需要按实例选择最合适的OR（MIP）表述以提升分配质量。

Method: 定义每个OR专家为一类MIP表述；构建平衡的专家条件SFT训练样本以学习输入—模式映射；用MIP求解器对历史实例进行求解并计算表述间的分配质量差，将差值转换为边际加权IPO偏好与每实例专家分数元数据；基于这些元数据在Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）中为候选响应分配奖励并训练LLM选择器，最终在真实工业数据上评估性能。

Result: 提出基于求解器引导的大模型（LLM）框架，先用专家条件的SFT构建schema学习，再利用求解器在历史实例上的评估将质量差转换为边际加权的IPO偏好与每实例专家得分元数据，借此在GRPO中分配奖励。实证上，GRPO在JD.com数据集上显著提高专家选择准确率和真实分配质量：Hit Ratio@1从21.45%提升到50.42%，Hit Ratio@2从70.47%提升到82.31%；对比现有基线，分配准确率增幅12.57个百分点，距事后最优（ex-post oracle）差距降至4.85个百分点。

Conclusion: 将求解器评估嵌入到选择器训练环路，并用GRPO对偏好进行强化化优化，可显著提升按实例选择MIP表述的效果，从而在实际分配任务上获得更高的目标值与更小的最优差距。

Abstract: Multi-warehouse inventory allocation is typically formulated as a mixed-integer programming (MIP) problem, yet no single formulation consistently matches heterogeneous instance-level regimes induced by demand concentration, inventory imbalance, replenishment scale, service constraints, and forecast volatility. We study this issue as instance-wise operations research (OR) formulation selection, where each allocation instance is assigned to a solver-executable formulation from a candidate OR expert library. We propose a solver-guided large language model (LLM) framework for OR formulation selection, in which each OR expert corresponds to a MIP formulation encoding a distinct allocation priority. To train the selector, the framework first constructs balanced expert-conditioned supervised fine-tuning (SFT) records for schema learning, and then uses MIP solver evaluation on historical instances to convert solver-evaluated allocation-quality gaps into margin-weighted identity preference optimization (IPO) preferences and per-instance expert-score metadata for reward lookup during group relative policy optimization (GRPO) to assign rewards to sampled responses. Experiments on multi-warehouse inventory allocation instances from JD$\mathord{.}$com, one of China's largest e-retailers, demonstrate that GRPO substantially improves expert-selection accuracy relative to the SFT+IPO selector and, more importantly, produces higher realized allocation quality than both the preference-trained selector and the best fixed formulation. With GRPO, Hit Ratio@1 and Hit Ratio@2 increase from 21.45% to 50.42% and from 70.47% to 82.31%. The resulting selector achieves an allocation accuracy gain of 12.57 percentage points over the incumbent baseline, outperforming both the SFT+IPO selector and the best fixed OR expert, and reduces the gap to the ex-post oracle to 4.85 percentage points.

</details>


### [110] [CHARM: A Multimodal Graph Foundation Model with Hierarchical Context Modeling for Zero-Shot Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26023)
*Ankang Yang,Jitao Zhao,Di Jin,Yuxiao Huang,Dongxiao He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CHARM：通过分层图上下文与模态感知的上下文编码器，把多模态图结构映射为能被 LLM 理解的图 token，从而增强零样本跨域迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界图通常为多模态且标签难以收集，现有基于 GNN 的图基座模型多依赖下游微调，基于 LLM 的方法又多局限于单一模态或单域任务，因此需要一种能在多模态图上实现零样本迁移的方法，既能提取单模态知识又能捕获可迁移的跨模态语义关系。

Method: CHARM 架构：用分层图上下文替换孤立原始节点，构建捕捉多模态语义与跨模态关系的上下文块；模态感知图上下文编码器融合节点的文本/图像等模态信息及图结构，将编码后的上下文表示转换成供大语言模型使用的“图 token”。模型目标是把领域特定的节点模式映射到共享的高层概念，从而实现无需目标域微调的零样本迁移。

Result: 作者声称 CHARM 在零样本多模态图任务上取得了稳定提升（abstract 中未给出具体数据集/指标），表明分层上下文与模态感知编码有助于提高跨域可迁移性。

Conclusion: 方法思路具有创新性，聚焦零样本多模态图迁移这一未充分探讨的方向；但 abstract 中缺乏关键实验细节和消融分析，需补充数据集、基线、任务、消融及计算代价等以支撑结论的稳健性。

Abstract: Graph foundation models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for transferring knowledge across graph domains and tasks. Real-world graphs associate nodes with text, images, and other modalities, making multimodal graphs essential for representing complex entities and relations. Moreover, collecting labels and adapting models for every new graph domain is costly and often infeasible, motivating zero-shot transfer. Unfortunately, zero-shot transfer on multimodal graphs remains underexplored. Existing GNN-based graph foundation models typically require downstream adaptation, whereas LLM-based graph methods mainly address unimodal graphs or tasks within a single domain. This setting presents two key challenges. First, models must generalize knowledge from individual modalities while capturing transferable cross-modal relations. Second, without target-domain fine-tuning, node representations remain entangled with domain-specific structures and modality-specific characteristics, obscuring shared concepts in unseen domains. To address these challenges, we propose CHARM, a multimodal graph foundation model with hierarchical context modeling for zero-shot transfer. CHARM replaces isolated raw nodes with hierarchical graph contexts that capture multimodal semantics and cross-modal relations. These contexts map domain-specific node patterns to shared high-level concepts, reducing reliance on target-domain supervision or adaptation. A modality-aware graph context encoder integrates multimodal information with graph structure and converts the resulting representations into graph tokens for a large language model . Experiments show consistent improvements on zero-shot multimodal graph tasks.

</details>


### [111] [Falling Behind Drives Unsafe Development in an Idealised AI Race Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26034)
*Elias Fernández Domingos,The Anh Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 实验与模型显示，不安全开发更多来源于早期行为动量、对手行为和害怕落后等战略性因素，而非个体风险偏好；政策应侧重缓解竞争压力并促进合作，而非仅关注个体风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察技术竞赛中速度与安全的张力，特别在人工智能领域，测试竞争压力是否促使开发者采取更高风险、更少关注安全的策略。

Method: 基于理想化的AI竞赛构造框架化行为实验：配对参与者在不确定时间长短的重复博弈中在Safe与Unsafe间选择；Unsafe带来更快进度与即时收益但会累积私有风险，风险上限设为10%、60%、90%三档；竞赛规则固定，仅变风险上限；同时收集个体风险偏好与行为序列；随后进行预注册与探索性数据分析，并构建一个包含四类策略的简化演化模型来解释观测到的行为模式。

Result: 预注册的不同风险上限之间的比较以及个体风险偏好对行为的假设均未得到数据支持。探索性分析表明，参与者选择“不安全”（Unsafe）更多由竞争态势演化驱动：对手的不安全选择会被模仿、领先减少不安全行为、落后增加不安全行为，首轮选择对后续行为有预测作用。提出的包含四类策略（始终安全、始终不安全、有条件安全、有条件反社会安全）的演化模型能重现观测到的效果，说明条件性不安全行为可由竞赛动态促进。

Conclusion: 不安全研发可以从战略相互作用中自发出现：早期选择与对手行为的“传染”效应以及相对落后驱动比个体风险偏好更关键。干预应以改变竞争结构、增强合作激励和降低落后惩罚为主。

Abstract: Technological races create tension between speed and safety: actors may gain by moving faster than competitors, even when risky development is harmful. This is prominent in debates about artificial intelligence (AI), where competitive pressure is often argued to incentivise riskier, less safety-conscious development. We study this using a framed behavioural experiment based on an idealised AI race, in which paired participants repeatedly chose between Safe and Unsafe development under an uncertain time horizon. Unsafe development gave faster progress and higher immediate payoffs but accumulated private risk up to a treatment-specific maximum of 10\%, 60\%, or 90\%; the race's competitive structure was held constant, and only this maximum risk varied. Neither the pre-registered comparison between risk levels nor the role of elicited risk preferences was supported by the data. Instead, exploratory analyses motivated by the task's repeated structure show that Unsafe behaviour is shaped less by risk preferences than by the evolving strategic state of the race: participants are more likely to choose Unsafe after their opponent does so, being ahead reduces Unsafe play while falling behind increases it, and first-round choices predict later behaviour. To interpret these effects we introduce a reduced evolutionary model with four strategies -- Always Safe, Always Unsafe, Conditionally Safe, and Conditionally Antisocial Safe -- which reproduces the treatment effect and shows how conditional Unsafe behaviour can be favoured by competitive race dynamics. Together, the experiment and model show that unsafe development can emerge from early behavioural momentum, opponent behaviour, and fear of falling behind, rather than from risk preferences alone, suggesting policy should focus on reducing competitive pressure and promoting cooperation in AI development rather than only individual risk.

</details>


### [112] [Desktop-Delta Bench: Do Computer-Use Models Understand Desktop GUI Transitions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26041)
*Abhishek Pillai,Samir Kumar Nayak,Yuan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DDB is a step-level, human-verified benchmark (2,013 instances) that diagnoses whether models can infer causal GUI state transitions; experiments across 8 model families reveal significant failure modes—ordering confusion, decoy susceptibility, and difficulty recognizing action families (drags), highlighting a missing diagnostic layer for desktop agent reliability.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Asynchronous desktop pipelines (inference, remote input, rendering, screenshot capture) can produce delayed, occluded, transient, or unrelated observations so next-frame evidence may be stale or misleading. Existing benchmarks focusing on final task success or single-frame grounding cannot isolate whether models truly infer the causal transitions produced by actions—necessary for verification, failure recovery, and rejecting stale observations.

Method: Construct an offline, human-verified step-level benchmark from 2,013 instances drawn from multi-app Linux trajectories (~15 apps, 50 task domains). Define two complementary tasks: (1) 463 3-frame temporal-ordering instances (including 105 cross-trajectory decoys) and (2) 1,550 before-after pairs labeled for 5 action families plus payload. Evaluate 8 model families across 32 ordering and 16 single-action settings, measuring exact-match and per-action metrics like F1 and localization quality.

Result: Ordering task remains unsaturated: best non-decoy and decoy exact-match rates ~65.1% and 65.7%. Task context improves decoy identification (+6.9 pp) but slightly reduces non-decoy exact-match (-2.2 pp); models systematically copy presented A-B-C order. For single-action prediction, action-family classification is harder than localization: click F1 ~0.96 vs drag F1 ~0.76, while recognized drags are localized well. Overall consistent performance gaps point to need for targeted verification and recovery improvements in CUAs.

Conclusion: Desktop-Delta Bench (DDB) fills a diagnostic gap between single-frame GUI grounding and end-to-end task success by testing whether models can reconstruct causal, task-relevant state transitions; results show substantial, consistent gaps across model families and tasks, indicating current models often misread or copy observations and struggle particularly on action-family inference (e.g., drags).

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) increasingly act through desktop GUIs to complete long-horizon tasks. Current benchmarks primarily measure end-task success or single-frame grounding. Neither isolates whether a model can reconstruct the causal, task-relevant transition produced by an action- crucial for rejecting stale observations, verifying progress, and recovering from failure. This is difficult because inference, remote input, app rendering, and screenshot capture are asynchronous: the next observation may be delayed, occluded, transient, or unrelated, then misread as progress and carried into subsequent planning. We introduce Desktop-Delta Bench (DDB), an offline step-level benchmark with 2,013 human-verified instances from novel, multi-app Linux trajectories across ~15 applications and 50 task domains. DDB trajectories targets 3 failure dimensions- state verification, source tracking, and context-aware control- through 2 complementary tasks: 463 3-frame temporal-ordering instances, including 105 with a cross-trajectory decoy, and 1,550 before-after pairs labeled from 5 actions + its payload. We evaluate 8 closed and open-source model families across 32 ordering and 16 single-action settings, observing consistent gaps. Ordering remains unsaturated: best non-decoy and decoy exact-match rates are 65.1% and 65.7%. Task context improves decoy identification by 6.9 percentage points but reduces non-decoy exact match by 2.2 points; error analysis reveals systematic copying of the presented A-B-C order. Single-action results show that inferring the action family is harder than locating it: click F1 is 0.96 vs, 0.76 for drag, while recognized drags are generally localized well. DDB, thus, complements end-to-end benchmarks by filling the missing diagnostic layer between GUI grounding and final task success, enabling targeted improvements to desktop CUA verification, reliability, and recovery.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Beyond "What to Retrieve": Uncertainty in Retrieval-Augmented Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24884)
*Chandan Kumar Sah,Xiaoli Lian,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 用不确定性作为可控信号对异构检索证据进行筛选和排序，从而提升仓库级代码生成的准确性，但效果依赖具体LLM后端和证据组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级代码生成依赖多种异构证据（相似代码、仓库上下文、项目API等），这些证据的相关性、兼容性和完整性不确定，现有RAG方法只优化检索相关性而未显式建模检索证据中的不确定性如何影响下游生成。

Method: 提出一个不确定性感知框架：为相似代码示例、仓库上下文、API集合等不同来源分别估计不确定性；基于不确定性对证据进行过滤和重排序；在生成阶段将不确定性作为控制信号，并用于后续的验证与自动修复流程；另外引入基于目标的API集合精化以提升API检索。并通过因子化实验分析各来源间交互以及对不同LLM后端的依赖性。

Result: 在扩展的32任务RepoExec-inline评测上，OpenCoder将GPT的selected-output正确率从基线RAG的56.25%提升到78.13%；其性能与一个verification-and-repair控制相当；在使用Gemini后未观测到统计学显著改进，显示收益依赖后端；目标感知的API精化显著提升了API集合检索效果。

Conclusion: OpenCoder通过对检索到的异构证据进行源特定不确定性估计并据此过滤、排序和引导生成/验证/修复，能在多个检索来源交互复杂的仓库级代码生成任务中显著提升基于GPT的输出正确率，但效果对后端模型敏感且不总优于强基准（verification-and-repair）。

Abstract: Repository-level code generation relies on heterogeneous evidence whose relevance, compatibility, and completeness are inherently uncertain. Similar-code examples, repository context, and project-specific APIs may provide complementary information, but can also introduce noisy, redundant, or conflicting signals. Existing retrieval-augmented approaches primarily optimize retrieval relevance without explicitly modeling how uncertainty in retrieved evidence affects downstream generation. We introduce OpenCoder, an uncertainty-aware framework that estimates source-specific uncertainty, uses it to filter and rank heterogeneous evidence, and guides generation, verification, and repair. A factorial analysis over API knowledge, repository context, and similar-code evidence reveals no universal additive source ranking; instead, significant cross-source interactions depend on the accompanying evidence and LLM backend. On an expanded 32-task RepoExec-inline evaluation, OpenCoder improves GPT selected-output correctness over Baseline RAG from 56.25\% to 78.13\%. However, it matches a verification-and-repair control, and the corresponding Gemini improvement is not statistically supported, indicating backend-dependent benefits. Target-aware API refinement also substantially improves API-set retrieval. These findings support treating uncertainty as an actionable control signal for repository-level retrieval, verification, and repair.

</details>


### [114] [Characterizing Structural Testability in JavaScript: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24965)
*Shahrzad Mirzaei,Saba Alimadadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对JavaScript的结构性可测性进行了大规模实证研究，基于AST静态分析提出七维度度量（可控性、可观测性、分支复杂度、异步协调、事件驱动行为、封装性、副作用强度），通过聚合为复合可测性得分（CTS）并在30个开源项目上验证，发现低CTS函数集中在少数文件且由多种结构模式导致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可测性研究多集中于面向对象或并发系统，未充分覆盖JavaScript特有的异步执行、事件驱动控制流、闭包与动态交互等结构性因素；需要专门的结构性可测性框架来理解和量化JavaScript中的测试难点。

Method: 基于抽象语法树（AST）进行静态特征提取，将7个维度的静态指标计算并标准化，聚合为复合可测性得分（CTS）。在30个不同领域与规模的开源项目上计算函数/文件/项目级别的CTS，采用分布分析、聚类/模式识别与关联分析来识别低CTS函数的结构原型和与项目属性的关系。

Result: 提出并实现了7维度CTS，实证表明低可测性函数在语义与结构上呈现若干反复出现的“难测”原型，且这些函数更多地聚集于少数文件。研究还揭示了CTS与项目特征之间存在可观察的关联，为后续自动化测试与重构技术指明了目标区域。

Conclusion: JavaScript的结构性可测性呈现聚集性问题——复杂/难测函数集中在少数文件中，而且没有单一的罪魁，而是多种结构配置反复出现。提出的七维CTS框架能量化这一现象，为测试生成、重构和质量评估提供实证基础。

Abstract: Software testability has long been recognized as a software quality attribute that influences testing effort and effectiveness. While prior work has extensively studied testability in object-oriented and concurrent software, comparatively little is known about structural testability in modern JavaScript systems. JavaScript applications rely on asynchronous execution, event-driven control flow, closures, and dynamic interactions that are not explicitly captured by existing testability frameworks. This paper presents a large-scale empirical study of structural testability in JavaScript. We operationalize structural testability as a seven-dimensional construct capturing controllability, observability, branching complexity, asynchronous coordination, event-driven behaviour, encapsulation, and side-effect intensity. These dimensions are derived from AST-based static analysis and aggregated into a Composite Testability Score (CTS) for comparative analysis across functions, files, and projects. We apply this framework to 30 open-source JavaScript projects spanning diverse domains and sizes. Our analysis characterizes the distribution of structural testability, identifies recurring structural archetypes among low-CTS functions, and examines associations between project characteristics and testability. We find that structurally-challenging functions are concentrated within a relatively small subset of files and arise through multiple recurring structural configurations rather than a single dominant pattern. These findings provide new insight into structural testability in JavaScript and establish a foundation for future research on testing effort, automated test generation, testability-aware refactoring, and software quality assessment.

</details>


### [115] [Preliminary Guidelines for Using and Evaluating GenAI Tools to Support Systematic Literature Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24991)
*Barbara Kitchenham,Sebastián Pizard,Lech Madeyski,Ronnie de Souza Santos,Martin Shepperd,David Budgen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Authors reviewed guidelines, used thought experiments and experience to create GUEST recommendations to guide trustworthy use and evaluation of GenAI in SLRs, concluding GenAI needs human oversight but can assist selectively.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper aims to support researchers who want to use Generative AI (GenAI) and Large Language Models (LLMs) in systematic literature reviews (SLRs) by addressing concerns about rigour, reliability, and transparency when applying these technologies to SLR tasks.

Method: A rapid review identified guideline studies; then authors combined thought experiments, existing literature guidance, and their own SLR and tool evaluation experience to develop process recommendations and discuss issues for planning, conducting, and reporting SLRs using GenAI.

Result: The paper produces a set of process recommendations named GUEST (GenAI Use and Evaluation in SLR Tasks), identifies problems and process issues for planning, conducting, and reporting SLRs using GenAI, and argues GenAI requires human oversight and cannot currently conduct unsupervised systematic studies, though it can assist with repetitive and validation tasks.

Conclusion: GenAI needs human supervision for SLRs and cannot perform unsupervised systematic studies; GUEST recommendations help ensure trustworthy SLRs and rigorous evaluations of GenAI tools.

Abstract: Context: Generative AI (GenAI) and Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for academic tasks in software engineering and beyond, including systematic literature reviews (SLRs). However, while capable of summarizing text, there is no guarantee they can meet the rigour, reliability, and transparency that SLRs require. Objectives: To support researchers intending to conduct SLRs using GenAI or those conducting empirical studies evaluating how well GenAI supports SLR tasks. Methods: First, we conducted a rapid review to identify studies that propose guidelines for evaluating and using GenAI and LLMs to support SLRs. Second, we drew on thought experiments, relevant guidance from the literature, and our own experience conducting SLRs and evaluating tools to develop recommendations for how to use and assess GenAI in the context of SLRs. Results: We discuss the problems researchers face when evaluating GenAI for SLRs. We identify and explain process issues to consider when planning, conducting, and reporting both SLRs using GenAI and evaluations of GenAI tools. Finally, we summarize our results as a set of process recommendations, which we name GUEST (GenAI Use and Evaluation in SLR Tasks). Conclusion: We argue that GenAI requires human oversight and is not currently capable of unsupervised systematic studies. However, it offers the prospect of cost-effective assistance for some repetitive tasks and for additional validation of some complex tasks. Our GUEST recommendations should help software engineering researchers both to conduct and report trustworthy SLRs using GenAI and to provide rigorous independent evaluation studies.

</details>


### [116] [Authoring Agent Skills: A Software-Engineering Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25032)
*Giuseppe Destefanis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 将Agent Skill视为软件制品，提出工程化设计原则、分阶段加载与行为评估的创作流程，并比较了与其它代理扩展机制的边界与选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多工具/多代理生态中，使Agent Skill作为可重用、可维护且高效的扩展单元，减少行为漂移与耦合，提升选择与加载效率，并规范第三方Skill的使用与信任管理。

Method: 以Claude Code为参考实现，结合规范性论证与结构性图示（UML、加载流程图、解剖图），并用比较分析法将Skill与其它机制定位；提出评估驱动的创作流程及常见模式/错误集合。

Result: 论文把“Agent Skill”定位为软件制品，主张按软件工程原则构建：单一职责、接口与实现分离、低耦合、在共享token预算下的经济性，并以行为评估替代确定性测试。以Claude Code为参考实现，说明了Skill的结构、分阶段加载机制、以及用于选择的描述如何编写；将Skill与项目记忆文件、斜杠命令、子代理、外部工具连接和hooks等机制比较，给出基于“谁决定机制运行”和“提供何种保证”的选择规则；提出以评估驱动的创作流程、常见模式与错误，并讨论第三方Skill的信任问题；文中用UML类图、加载模型、Skill构造解剖图及运行时插入点来说明。

Conclusion: 主张把Skill按软件工程最佳实践设计与组织，通过描述驱动的选择、分阶段加载和基于行为的评估提升可复用性与可靠性，同时提出对第三方Skill的审查与信任需要解决。

Abstract: Agent Skills are an emerging way to extend large language model agents with reusable procedural knowledge that the agent loads on demand. Anthropic introduced Agent Skills and published the format as an open specification supported across several agent tools. This note argues that a skill is a software artefact and that its construction should follow software-engineering principles, with qualifications: single responsibility, separation of interface from implementation, low coupling, and economy in a shared token budget, together with behavioural evaluation in place of deterministic testing. Using Claude Code as the reference implementation, it describes how a skill is structured, how its contents are loaded in stages, and how to write the description on which selection depends. It places skills against the other mechanisms a developer can use to shape agent behaviour, like project memory files, slash commands, subagents, external tool connections, and hooks, and gives a rule for choosing between them based on who decides that a mechanism runs and what guarantee it provides. It then sets out an evaluation-driven authoring process, a set of patterns and faults commonly encountered in authoring, and the trust question raised by using skills from third parties. We illustrate the comparison drawn in UML class style, the loading model, the anatomy of a skill, the relative position of each mechanism, and the points at which skills and hooks act during a session.

</details>


### [117] [What Motivates Whom? A Survey of Newcomers to OSS and Experienced OSS Practitioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25126)
*Shashiwadana Nirmani,Hourieh Khalajzadeh,Mojtaba Shahin,Xiao Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对208名从业者的在线问卷，研究揭示人口统计与动机相关联，不同动机对应不同的项目偏好，并比较了新手与有经验参与者以指导推荐系统与入门支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解贡献者如何选择OSS项目，以便优化入门流程、提高参与者匹配度与长期参与、并为基于动机的项目推荐系统提供依据。

Method: 在线问卷（n=208），收集人口统计、动机、项目特征偏好及对推荐系统的看法；采用相关性分析并分别对新手与有经验参与者进行比较分析。

Result: 调查发现：参与者的动机与人口统计学特征（年龄、性别、OSS角色）显著相关；不同动机的参与者对项目特征（如项目年龄、开发阶段、文档质量等）有不同偏好；并将新手与有经验参与者分开分析，以揭示二者的不同需求；最后收集了参与者对现有项目推荐系统改进方向的观点。

Conclusion: 研究为OSS社区和项目推荐系统提供了可操作的、以动机为中心的设计建议，有助于改善新手入门、提升参与度与保留率，但结果受限于自报数据与样本代表性。

Abstract: Open source software (OSS) development continues to expand, yet software practitioners often struggle to select suitable projects, leading to inefficient onboarding and disengagement. Understanding how contributors select OSS projects is important for supporting contributors onboarding, engagement, and long-term participation within OSS communities. This study investigates contributors' project-selection preferences in OSS projects and examines how these preferences correlate with contributors' motivations and demographic backgrounds. Through an online survey of 208 practitioners, we found that demographic factors, such as age, gender, and the OSS role they held, significantly correlate with their motivations. Additionally, preferences for project characteristics such as project age, development stage, and documentation quality vary based on specific motivations. Importantly, our findings are presented through a comparative lens, analyzing the responses of newcomers to OSS and experienced OSS practitioners separately to uncover their distinct preferences. Lastly, we explore software practitioners' perspectives on how existing recommendation systems could better support project selection and align with their motivations. By disentangling the unique needs of newcomers to OSS and OSS practitioners, our findings provide insights for researchers, OSS project owners, and software practitioners to improve contributor onboarding, engagement, and retention, while also informing future project recommendation systems and improving the OSS ecosystem.

</details>


### [118] [Learning from 53.6K Real-World Developer Edits of AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25130)
*Jenny T. Liang,Mihika Bairathi,Wayne Chi,Ameet Talwalkar,Nishant Subramani,Valerie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 作者构建了DECODE数据集，包含53.6K条真实的IDE中对AI生成代码的编辑（Python/TypeScript/JavaScript），并分析编辑行为与时间分布，发现多数编辑在接受补全后15分钟内发生，31%轨迹最终移除AI补全；将DECODE用于微调可显著提升开源3B模型的代码编辑预测能力，优于前沿LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多以公开Git提交为训练数据，但Git提交反映的是最终成功代码，缺乏真实、细粒度的人工修补轨迹；需要面向开发者的编辑数据以改进AI编程助手的实用性与预测能力。

Method: 从1K+开发者的IDE会话收集53.6K条对AI生成代码的实时编辑轨迹（Python/TS/JS）；对编辑时间、编辑类型和结果（例如补全被移除的比率）进行统计分析；将DECODE用于微调若干LLM并在代码编辑预测任务上进行基准比较。

Result: 数据分析显示多数编辑在15分钟内发生，31%编辑轨迹移除AI补全；在模型评估中，使用DECODE微调后的开源3B模型在编辑预测任务上显著优于未微调的前沿LLM，证明开发者级编辑数据的价值。

Conclusion: DECODE是一份高质量、开发者中心的代码编辑数据集，既可用于行为分析也能显著提升小型开源模型在代码编辑预测任务上的表现，强调未来AI编程助手应以开发者编辑数据为训练资源。

Abstract: Imperfections in AI-generated code require that software developers modify the generated code manually, or by re-prompting an AI programming assistant. Manual code edits provide more realistic and granular information on editing behavior than Git commits, which only contain final successful code snippets. Yet, due to a lack of high-quality, realistic code editing data, LLMs are mostly trained on publicly available Git data (e.g., commits). To address this gap, we introduce DECODE (Developer Edits of Code Dataset), a dataset of 53.6K real-world in-IDE code edits of AI-generated code in Python, TypeScript, and JavaScript, sourced from 1K+ developers. First, we demonstrate the utility of DECODE for data analysis, obtaining insights on when, why, and how AI-generated code is edited. We find that most edits occur within the first 15 minutes after accepting an AI completion, resulting in the removal of AI completions in 31% of edit trajectories. Second, we use DECODE to benchmark the ability of LLMs to predict code edits. We find that finetuning on DECODE enables open-source 3B models to perform code edit prediction tasks significantly better than frontier LLMs. We then discuss implications of this work, emphasizing the necessity of developer-centric machine learning approaches for future AI programming assistants.

</details>


### [119] [Specification-Driven DevOps for Multi-Service Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25141)
*Oleg Grynets,Kyrylo Fursov,Vasyl Lyashkevych,Volodymyr Veres*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLM 能从代码库推断出能跑起来的部署，但经常错过关键的部署意图细节；功能正确性≠部署意图一致性，需要在仓库中显式补充最小部署规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估前沿大语言模型在缺乏开发者部署制品（如 Dockerfile、docker-compose）的情况下，能否仅凭代码库工件自动生成多服务应用的可执行容器化部署，并判断可执行性是否等同于符合部署意图（架构、安全、工作流、生产约束）。

Method: 使用一款前沿 LLM 生成 Docker/Docker Compose，从包含 Python、Node.js、.NET、React、Rust、Java、Redis、PostgreSQL、MySQL 兼容基础设施的三个异质仓库出发，采用确定性的端到端 HTTP 响应判定（oracles）和人工结构比对来验证功能与结构差异；记录并分析缺失的部署意图要素与一次基镜像需更新的修正。

Result: 在三个异质仓库上，模型生成的 Dockerfiles 与 Docker Compose 配置均能使环境功能上可运行；模型正确重构了服务拓扑、端口、基础设施依赖、主机名、后台任务、隐含代理配置与基于文件的 Docker secrets。但模型反复遗漏网络分段、多阶段构建、依赖层缓存、热重载卷、面向生产的前端部署、严格的后端端口策略和跨平台构建逻辑；另有一次需将 Rust 基镜像从 1.85 升级到 1.88 才能运行。

Conclusion: 研究形式化区分了功能正确性与部署意图一致性，并推导出一组最小显式部署规范（例如镜像版本、网络分段、构建分阶段与平台目标、端口与访问策略、运行时卷与热重载、秘密管理与生产级前端服务），以填补从代码无法可靠推断的信息空白。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate executable software environments from repository artifacts. However, functional executability does not necessarily imply conformity with architectural, security, workflow, and production intent. This study investigates whether a frontier LLM can generate Dockerfiles and Docker Compose configurations for multi-service applications using repository contents without access to developer-authored deployment artifacts. Three heterogeneous repositories combining Python, Node.js, .NET, React, Rust, Java, Redis, PostgreSQL, and MySQL-compatible infrastructure were evaluated using deterministic end-to-end HTTP oracles and manual structural comparison. All three generated environments became functionally operational, although one required a Rust base-image update from version 1.85 to 1.88. The model correctly reconstructed service topology, application ports, infrastructure dependencies, service hostnames, a background worker, hidden proxy configurations, and a file-based Docker secrets mechanism. However, it consistently omitted network segmentation, multi-stage builds, dependency-layer caching, live-reload volumes, production frontend serving, restrictive backend-port policies, and cross-platform build logic. Based on these observations, the study formalizes the distinction between functional correctness and deployment-intent fidelity and analytically derives a minimal explicit deployment specification for information that cannot be reliably inferred from repository artifacts.

</details>


### [120] [NFR-to-Code Traceability in a Blockchain-IoT System: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25325)
*Yifei Wang,Jacky Keung,Xiaoxue Ma,Shijie Zhang,Yishu Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: The paper builds a curated FR/NFR traceability subset for a blockchain-IoT project and shows standard IR methods struggle more on NFRs—especially security—because relevant implementation evidence is dispersed across code artifacts.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing requirement-to-code traceability research focuses mainly on functional requirements; non-functional requirements (NFRs) are harder to trace and underexplored, limiting verification of qualities like security and reliability.

Method: Designed an annotation protocol to create trace links across heterogeneous artifacts and manually annotated a subset containing both FR and NFR links; analyzed NFR trace characteristics; evaluated TF-IDF, BM25, LSI, and WMD retrieval baselines on the subset.
